# SeeWe Platform Worklog

---
Task ID: 2-b
Agent: general-purpose (AI Interview Engine)
Task: AI interview backend (question generation, answer evaluation, TTS/ASR, reports)

Work Log:
- Read project context: prisma/schema.prisma (Interview/Answer/Shortlist/Application/Job/Client models, no enums), src/lib/db.ts, src/lib/types.ts, package.json, and the z-ai-web-dev-sdk@0.0.18 type declarations/dist to confirm exact API surface (chat.completions.create w/ thinking disabled; audio.tts.create returns raw fetch Response -> .arrayBuffer(); audio.asr.create returns parsed JSON with .text).
- Created src/lib/ai-client.ts: singleton getZAI(), robust extractJson<T>() (strips ```json fences, finds first balanced {...}/[...] block string/escape-aware, tolerates trailing commas, throws 'AI returned invalid JSON'), DEFAULT_MAX_RETRIES=2 with withLLMRetry exponential backoff 500ms*attempt, chat(system,user) helper returning completion.choices[0]?.message?.content ?? '', plus chatWithRetry().
- Created POST /api/interview/start: loads shortlist incl application{candidate{skills,resumeText,headline},job}; 404 if missing; idempotent resume path returns existing interview (questions parsed) + resumed:true; difficulty validated EASY|MEDIUM|HARD default MEDIUM; LLM prompt (expert interviewer persona, STRICT JSON array of exactly 6 items {question,focusArea,modelAnswerPoints[]}, resume clipped 1500 chars / JD 1200 chars, mix = 1 intro + 4 technical + 1 problem-solving); normalizer enforces qIndex 0..5 and tops up short sets from a generic domain/skill pool; full template fallback set (references jobTitle + top skills) if AI fails after retries; atomic $transaction creates Interview(status IN_PROGRESS, startedAt) + shortlist INTERVIEW_IN_PROGRESS + application INTERVIEW_SCHEDULED; returns data.interview with questions as PARSED objects.
- Created GET /api/interview/[id]: async params ({ params }: Promise<{id:string}>); loads interview incl answers ordered by qIndex asc, shortlist.client, shortlist.application.job{id,title,company}; parses questions & report JSON strings safely; 404 handling; response { data }.
- Created POST /api/interview/answer: validates interviewId/qIndex range/COMPLETED guard; upserts Answer base via compound key interviewId_qIndex keeping question text; evaluation prompt gives question+focusArea+modelAnswerPoints+transcript(<=4000 chars), expects STRICT JSON {score 0-10 one decimal, feedback 2-3 sentences, strengths[], improvements[]}; empty/<15 alnum-char transcripts skip LLM -> score 1 "Candidate did not provide a meaningful answer."; scores clamped [0,10]; LLM failure -> score null + neutral feedback; returns {...stored strings, parsedStrengths, parsedImprovements}.
- Created /api/interview/report: POST computes overallScore (avg of scored answers, .1 rounding) + verdict mapping (>=8 STRONG_HIRE, >=6.5 HIRE, >=5 MAYBE, else NO_HIRE); LLM report call fed Q&A pairs expects STRICT JSON {aiSummary<=90 words, dimensionScores EXACTLY 4 named dims, strengths 3-4, weaknesses 2-3, recommendation}; sanitizer forces the 4 dimension names & minimums, deterministic fallback report if AI fails; atomic $transaction updates interview(COMPLETED, completedAt, report JSON)+shortlist(COMPLETED)+application(INTERVIEWED); returns data with report PARSED. GET ?clientId=|recruiterId= lists COMPLETED interviews ('ALL_CLIENTS' accepted => no client filter; recruiterId filters through job.recruiterId via Prisma.InterviewWhereInput), maps to {interviewId,candidateName,candidateEmail,jobTitle,company,clientName,clientEmail,overallScore,verdict,aiSummary,completedAt,interviewUrlHint:'/'}, sorted completedAt desc.
- Created POST /api/tts: trims text, graceful clip at sentence boundary near 900 chars (hard cut + ellipsis fallback); voice whitelist ['tongtong','chuichui','xiaochen','jam','kazi','douji','luodo'] default 'jam'; zai.audio.tts.create({input,voice,speed:1.0,response_format:'wav',stream:false}) -> Buffer -> base64 WAV returned as 'data:audio/wav;base64,' inside { data }; in-memory Map cache (text-hash per voice) capped at 60 entries w/ recency refresh; errors logged + 502 { error:'Speech generation failed' }.
- Created POST /api/asr: accepts file_base64 or audioBase64 alias; strips data-URL prefix on 'base64,' marker; 413 friendly error > ~12MB base64; zai.audio.asr.create({file_base64}) -> { data: response.text }; empty/error results -> console.error + 422 { error:'Could not transcribe audio. Please type your answer.' }.
- Verification: ran scoped tsc --noEmit (temp tsconfig in /tmp extending project config, only the 7 new files + lib deps included) - all new files compile clean against generated Prisma client. NOTE pre-existing unrelated error in src/lib/types.ts line 99 (`CandidateSummary` name used but never defined in Application.candidate?) - file owned by another task, flagged for its owner.

Stage Summary:
- Artifacts: src/lib/ai-client.ts, src/app/api/interview/start/route.ts, src/app/api/interview/[id]/route.ts, src/app/api/interview/answer/route.ts, src/app/api/interview/report/route.ts, src/app/api/tts/route.ts, src/app/api/asr/route.ts.
- Full pipeline: start(resume-aware, AI questions w/ 2-level fallback) -> TTS voice questions (wav data URLs, cached) -> candidate speech via ASR -> per-answer LLM grading -> final rated report shared to clients (GET list).
- No files outside the allowed list touched (types.ts CandidateSummary issue pre-exists; recommended fix by types owner: export interface CandidateSummary or inline the shape).

LEFT pane: sticky self-camera <video autoPlay muted playsInline> height min(40vw,224px) (getUserMedia video-only best-effort; denial/unavailability → gradient-mesh pulsing bot placeholder 'Camera off — voice or typed answers welcome'), REC pill + per-recording timer, AnalyserNode canvas bars (raf loop, roundRect fallback fillRect), progress card w/ answered count Progress + navigator dots (emerald check answered / ping-ring current / dashed hollow upcoming); RIGHT pane: question card (Question x of y, focusArea teal chip, text-lg prompt) and answer stack — toggle 🎙 Record Answer (MediaRecorder audio/webm when supported else default; chunks→FileReader readAsDataURL→POST /api/asr fills editable textarea + 'Voice captured' badge; blob too small warns; 422/network failure → toast.warn('Transcription unavailable — please type below') gracefully), always-visible Textarea 'Speak or type your answer…', Submit Answer ➜ guards empty input; scoring phase = shimmer sweep card ('AI evaluating…' skeleton lines) then POST /api/interview/answer reveals spring pop-in SCORE CARD: banded color block ≥7 emerald / ≥4.5 amber / else rose, big score/10, feedback paragraph, strengths chips (Plus icon emerald), improvements chips (Wrench amber), fairness-gated <details> modelAnswerPoints hints ONLY post-score, [Next Question ➜] or last-question [Finish & Generate Report] (gradient 48px CTAs, all controls ≥44px); finalize view cycles staged messages ('Analyzing technical depth…','Calibrating communication score…','Comparing against JD benchmarks…') under animated Progress while POST /api/interview/report runs, retry path on failure, success mounts full AIReportCard + [Exit to Dashboard] + bumpRefresh(); resume edge cases covered (already-complete interviews reopen onto finish card).
- Created src/components/seewee/candidate/CandidateDashboard.tsx (named export CandidateDashboard): signed-out friendly gate card (setAuthOpen(true)); Tabs 'My Applications' | 'My Profile'; applications fetched via GET /api/applications?candidateId= keyed on user.id + store.refreshKey w/ skeletons, error+retry, and empty→BrowseJobs states; per-application card = company-initial gradient Avatar, job title/company/location/workMode, formatLpa salary, date-fns timeAgo, match% badge colored by band; 5-stage pipeline tracker Applied✓→Shortlisted→AI Interview→Client Review→Offer (done stages emerald CheckCircle2 + solid connectors, current stage emerald ping dot, pending gray dashed circles, REJECTED dims tracker + rose dot); IN_PROGRESS interview & view!=='interview' renders pulsing emerald→teal [Resume AI Interview] banner that dispatches CustomEvent('seewee:start-interview', detail: shortlists[0]?.id ?? application.id) for the shell listener (documented in code comment); COMPLETED shows verdict chip emoji+label+overallScore/10 via VERDICT_META; REPORT_SHARED/SHARED_WITH_CLIENT shows 'Shared with <client>' send-badge with emerald glow; Profile tab = full editable form (name phone headline location skills CSV experienceYears expectedSalary education resumeText w/ char counter + thin custom webkit scrollbar class), auto-filled from store user then layered over localStorage 'seewee-draft-profile'; save tries PATCH /api/candidates/:userId exactly as contracted — on !ok/network catch it stores the local draft, flips a persistent '(draft saved locally – sync coming soon)' amber note, toast.info fallback, never crashes (route missing today works by design); success path syncs into store via setUser + clears draft.
- Verification: scoped tsc --noEmit over just the 3 new files (temp tsconfig extending project config) → 0 errors after fixing: missing JSX brace in mute-badge ternary, invalid narrowed `phase === 'scoring'` comparison in listening branch (replaced with transcribing||recording guard), stale srcObject attach race (video now re-attached via effect once it mounts), illegal react namespace types (switched to imported ReactNode/FormEvent), plus removed bogus placeholder tail. eslint clean on all three files; no other project file touched.

Stage Summary:
- Artifacts: src/components/seewee/candidate/CandidateDashboard.tsx, src/components/seewee/interview/InterviewRoom.tsx, src/components/seewee/interview/AIReportCard.tsx (only files created).
- Interview UX end-to-end: start/resume → spoken questions w/ graceful reading-mode fallback → voice ASR or typing → instant per-answer graded score cards w/ fairness-gated hints → staged report generation → shareable verdict report card. Every media/browser API wrapped in try/catch + typeof guards; permission denial degrades to placeholders, never crashes.

- Created MyJobsPanel.tsx (named export): responsive 1/2/3-col card grid (status color-coded badges ACTIVE emerald-outline / PAUSED amber-outline / CLOSED slate), meta rows (location/exp range/formatLpa salary/posted distance/viewsCount), skills slice(0,5)+n-more chips, clickable applicants count badge -> onSelectJobForApplicants(jobId); DropdownMenu actions Pause/Activate/Close via PATCH /api/jobs/[id] {status} and Delete gated behind a real shadcn AlertDialog confirm; skeletons mirror card shapes; empty state pushes the Post-a-Job CTA.

- Created RecruiterDashboard.tsx (named export): mounted-gate skeleton hydration guard + signed-out gate card (setAuthOpen(true)); hero strip emerald→teal gradient w/ greeting/time-of-day/company/date + 4 glassy stat tiles (Active Jobs, Total Applicants, Shortlisted= PIPELINE_BEYOND_APPLIED statuses, AI Interviews Done from report count) refreshed on store refreshKey; Tabs 'My Jobs | Applicants | Talent Search | Client Reports | Clients' (Clients last) with Post-a-Job white-on-gradient button opening max-w-2xl scrollable Dialog hosting PostJobForm; MyJobs applicant-badge click lifts {jobId,n} focusRequest -> auto-switches tab & ApplicantsPanel focuses that job filter (nonce makes repeat clicks re-fire). Task ID: cron-review-2
Agent: Z.ai Code (automated review cycle)
Task: QA sweep + Job Alerts feature + Recruiter Edit Job + Withdraw application + styling polish

Work Log:
- STATUS ASSESSMENT: lint PASS, tsc src 0 errors, dev.log all 200s. agent-browser QA of home / jobs grid / recruiter hub (stats, charts, applicants, talent search) / candidate journey (pipeline tracker, verdict chip) — all rendering, no console errors, no blocking bugs found.
- NEW FEATURE — Job Alerts (signature feature) end-to-end:
  * Prisma model JobAlert (userId cascade, keyword, location, minSalaryLpa?, lastSeenAt, @@index userId) + db:push. NOTE: dev server needed a restart to pick up the new Prisma client (recurring SQLite/Next quirk).
  * /api/job-alerts: GET (alerts enriched with newCount = ACTIVE jobs created after lastSeenAt matching keyword against title+skills+description, location substring, salary floor; totalMatches; sample top-4 jobs), POST (validated, dupe-guard per user+kw+loc, fresh alert starts with lastSeenAt=now), PATCH (mark seen, owner-checked), DELETE (owner-checked).
  * JobAlertsBell.tsx in Header (candidate only): bell with unread badge (sum of newCounts, 9+ cap), 60s polling, Popover listing alerts w/ "N new" badges + newest sample job + "latest X ago", closing marks all seen (optimistic + PATCH), Manage → dispatches 'seewee:candidate-tab' {tab:'alerts'} + setView('candidate').
  * JobAlertsTab.tsx in CandidateDashboard (new "Alerts" tab, TabsList now grid-cols-4 + controlled value state): create form (keyword/location/min LPA, validation, 409 dupe toast) + alert cards (new/caught-up badges, total matches, salary floor, sample job rows, Browse matches, delete).
  * JobsView header CTA "Alert me about these jobs" (auth-gate; recruiters get "Go to Hiring Hub"; requires non-empty filters else info toast).
  * VERIFIED in browser: create via form (toast + reset + live sample), bell badge "1" appears after recruiter posts matching job, popover shows "1 new" + latest job, close → mark seen (API verified new:1→0), manage tab renders, delete works.
- NEW FEATURE — Recruiter Edit Job:
  * PostJobForm refactored: optional editJob prop (prefills all 12 fields via initialState), mode switches copy/icon/submit ("Update this role", "💾 Save changes") and PATCHes /api/jobs/[id] instead of POST; create path unchanged (verified dialog still renders empty + "🚀 Post Job").

Task ID: cron-review-3-b
Agent: general-purpose (Resume AI Parser)
Task: Resume upload/paste + AI parse into candidate profile

Work Log:
- Read ai-client.ts (chat/chatWithRetry/extractJson), PATCH /api/candidates/[id] whitelist (name, phone, headline, location, skills [CSV string], experienceYears, currentSalary, expectedSalary, education, resumeText, company) and CandidateDashboard profile-tab conventions before writing code.
- NEW POST /api/resume/parse: body {resumeText}; 400 if trimmed <30 chars; clips at 12000 chars. Strict system prompt ("You are an expert resume parser…") demanding JSON-only {headline, skills[5-12], experienceYears:number(0 if unclear), location|null, currentCompany|null, education|null, summary(max 60 words), suggestedCategories[2-4]}; LLM call via chatWithRetry + extractJson + defensive normalizers (trim/dedupe/cap skills, clamp years 0-60, clipWords 60). Quality gate → falls back to deterministic heuristicParser on any AI failure/empty output (never 500 for AI hiccups): ~64-entry curated tech-skill list with lookaround word-boundary matching (handles C++/.NET/Next.js), /\d+(\.\d)?\s*\+?\s*(years?|yrs?)/ max-extract for experienceYears, first-meaningful-line→70-char headline, Indian-cities list (+ "Location :" labeled line) with Bangalore/Gurgaon canonicalization, degree regex for education, category suggester from skills. Response contract: { data: { extracted: {...}, source: 'ai' | 'heuristic' } }.
- NEW ResumeParserCard.tsx (client): emerald/teal card, Paste text / Upload file toggle tabs (Paste = Textarea min-h-[140px] + "x / 12,000" counter; Upload = accept .txt,.md,.csv,.json,.text, client-side file.text(), graceful toasts for >200KB/binary/unsupported ext — PDF hint shown, no PDF parsing); "✨ Analyze with AI" (min-h-11, disabled <30 chars) → animated "Reading your resume…" pulse placeholder; review step shows editable Inputs (headline/location/experienceYears/currentCompany/education) + removable skill chips w/ add-your-own input and custom thin scrollbar + non-editable suggestedCategories badges + summary muted block; per-field apply checkboxes (all default-checked; null/empty fields hidden), separate "Also save the full resume text" checkbox (default checked); Apply button PATCHes only checked fields (currentCompany→company mapping) + resumeText when checked via /api/candidates/{id}; success toast 'Profile updated with AI-extracted details ✨' → onApplied() → collapse to input mode; all errors toast server message; ARIA labels on icon buttons.
- EDIT CandidateDashboard.tsx (integration only): import card; added reloadProfileFromServer() (GETs /api/candidates/{id}, setUsers fresh values AND refills ProfileDraftState identically to the mount effect + clears stale localStorage draft) wired as onApplied; inserted an "Instant resume import" heading + <ResumeParserCard candidateId={user.id}> at top of the Profile TabsContent, above the existing profile form.
- Verified: scoped tsc (/home/z/tmp-verify tsconfig extending project, only 3 files) → 0 errors; full-tree tsc errors limited to vendored skills/examples dirs (src clean); eslint on 3 files → clean; API tests vs dev server: realistic resume → source:'ai', correct shape (headline/skills/experienceYears/location/company/education/summary/categories); garbage text → source:'heuristic'; <30 chars → 400 w/ message; 13k-char input clipped & processed; PATCH round-trip confirmed exact card payload accepted (then restored Meghna's seed location/education/resumeText/company:null).
- dev.log after root-page recompile: GET / 200 compile ok; zero "Failed to compile"; only intentional heuristic-fallback console.error from route (by design).

- Candidates can now paste or upload their resume (plain-text formats ≤200KB), have AI extract headline/skills/experience/location/company/education/summary/job-categories into a reviewable form, and apply selected fields straight onto their profile — with a deterministic fallback whenever the LLM misbehaves.
- API contract: POST /api/resume/parse {resumeText} → 200 {data:{extracted:{headline,skills[],experienceYears,location,currentCompany,education,summary,suggestedCategories[]}, source}} | 400 (<30 chars). Parse-only by design — persistence happens through existing PATCH /api/candidates/[id].
- Integration point: CandidateDashboard Profile tab, top of tab under an "Instant resume import" heading; onApplied re-syncs store user + form draft from GET /api/candidates/[id].

Agent: Z.ai Code (automated review cycle)
Task: QA sweep + REAL VIDEO ANSWERS end-to-end (record → persist → stream → replay) + CSV per-question score columns + recording UI polish

Work Log:
- STATUS ASSESSMENT: eslint PASS, tsc 0 errors (src), dev.log all 200s, zero console errors. agent-browser QA: marketing home, Find Jobs (cards/salary), recruiter sign-in → Hiring Hub (stats, both charts, funnel, client decisions), analytics all healthy. No blocking bugs → proceeded to the #1 backlog item: interviews were still audio-only despite the product promising "interviews on video".
- NEW FLAGSHIP FEATURE — Real video answers, end-to-end (record → store → stream → replay in every transcript):
  * Prisma: Answer gains mediaPath (filename under upload/interview-media/), mediaKind ("video"|"audio"), mediaSeconds. db:push OK; dev server restarted for client regen (first post-change API test 500'd on the stale client — known pattern, pkill+restart fixed).
  * POST /api/interview/answer now accepts optional {media_base64 (data URL or raw), mediaSeconds}: saveMedia() validates size (12MB base64 cap), decodes, sniffs the EBML magic (1A 45 DF A3) so only real webm lands on disk, writes upload/interview-media/{interviewId}-q{qIndex}-{ts}.webm, and persists mediaPath/mediaKind/mediaSeconds on the graded Answer in the same update. Media save failure is logged & skipped — never fails the graded answer. COMPLETED-interview guard unchanged.
  * NEW GET /api/interview/media/[file]: streams recordings with FULL HTTP Range support (206 + Content-Range) so <video> seeking works; strict filename allow-list (^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\.webm$) + resolved-path prefix check blocks traversal (404 human copy when a pre-media answer has no file). Content-Type video/webm, private cache.
  * InterviewRoom: toggleRecording() now folds the LIVE CAMERA TRACK into the recorded stream (MediaStream([mic audio..., camera video...])) whenever the preview is on → MediaRecorder picks video/webm;codecs=vp8,opus (fallbacks vp9 → plain video/webm → audio/webm when camera denied). handleRecordingStop() converts the take ONCE to a data URL, stashes it as pendingMedia {base64, kind, seconds} (kind from rec.mimeType), then tries ASR — on ASR failure with video the take is KEPT with softer copy ("Recording kept, but transcription failed — please type"). submitAnswer() sends media_base64+mediaSeconds with the transcript (oversize takes are dropped with a heads-up, not a failure), clearing pendingMedia on success; gotoQuestion() clears it too. Audio-only takes (camera off) are ALSO stored now → audio playback for camera-denied candidates.
  * InterviewTranscript (all 4 surfaces auto-inherit: recruiter Applicants dialog, Client Reports dossier, client portal, candidate done screen): answers with media render a real player — video (aspect-video, rounded-2xl, slate-950 chrome, native controls, playsInline) or audio (dark gradient strip + icon + <audio>), each with a figcaption strip ("Recorded video/audio answer" + duration m:ss). Per-q onError fallback chip "Recording no longer available"; header subtitle now reports "N recordings attached" when present; answer meta line says "video answer · 0:42" vs "voice answer" vs "typed". Print-aware: players tagged data-print-hide + a print-only "🎬 Recorded answer available in the SeeWe app." note so PDF exports stay clean.
  * Candidate done screen: new "Review your answers" section under the AI report embedding InterviewTranscript — candidates can immediately rewatch their own takes + read per-question AI notes (previously report was scores-only).
- NEW FEATURE — CSV export per-question scores: GET /api/interview/report items now include questionScores (number|null[] in qIndex order, additive); ApplicantsPanel CSV gains Q1–Q6 Score columns (blank when unanswered) → analysts can see question-level performance in Excel.
- STYLING DETAILS (mandatory): REC pill next to the submit row while recording (rose border/bg, pulsing dot, "REC video/audio · 0:12", aria-live); camera tile gets a rose ring + border glow while recording (rec indicator now reads "Rec · video 0:12"); "Video captured · 7s" teal badge vs "Voice captured · 7s" emerald badge post-capture; player chrome (rounded, dark, captioned) matches the app's card language.
- VERIFIED (API + browser E2E):
  * scripts/test-media.ts harness (kept for reuse): seeds a throwaway user→application→shortlist→interview chain, POSTs an answer WITH a synthetic webm → 200 + mediaPath/kind/seconds persisted; FULL GET 200 video/webm len correct; RANGE GET 206 "bytes 100-199/2052"; traversal path → 400; missing file → 404; transcript payload carries media fields; cascade cleanup + file delete. (One orphan file from the pre-restart failed run removed.)
  * Demo data: ffmpeg-generated 3s 640×360 vp8+opus test-pattern webm attached to Meghna's completed interview Q1 (138KB) so the feature demos out-of-the-box.
  * Browser: recruiter → Client Reports → Meghna → Open Full Dossier → transcript header "1 recording attached", <video> readyState=4 640×360 with native controls (screenshot); candidate Meghna → completed strip → done screen "Review your answers" → same video playable (screenshot); mobile 390px: player 257px wide, section headers wrap cleanly; exit routing back to "Welcome back, Meghna"; console clean throughout; dev.log shows real 206 range requests from playback.
  * Final: eslint PASS, tsc 0 errors (src), viewport reset + browser closed.
- NOTE: eval-based .click() doesn't toggle Radix tabs (use agent-browser's native click with refs) — NOT an app bug; cost a few debug minutes this round.

Stage Summary:
- SeeWe's core promise is now literally true: candidates answer ON VIDEO (camera track folded into MediaRecorder when the preview is live, graceful audio-only fallback), recordings are stored on disk and streamed with seek support, and EVERYONE in the hiring loop — recruiter, client, and the candidate themselves — can play the actual footage next to the transcript, the score, and the AI's reasoning. Audio-only takes are stored too, so camera-denied candidates still get playback.
- New contracts: Answer.mediaPath/mediaKind/mediaSeconds (db pushed); POST /api/interview/answer accepts media_base64+mediaSeconds; NEW GET /api/interview/media/[file] (Range-enabled); GET /api/interview/report items include questionScores; AnswerRecord/SubmitAnswerRequest extended; InterviewTranscript unchanged API (media renders automatically); candidate done screen adds transcript section.
- Demo data state: Meghna's Senior React interview Q1 now has a playable 3s test-pattern video (demo artifact); 10 jobs, 6 applicants, 2 completed reports unchanged otherwise.
- Next-round candidates: recorder quality options (resolution/bitrate cap + 2-min soft limit), recruiter-side download of recordings, interview-room network-loss retry for media upload, saved-search chips from bell items, dark-mode via semantic tokens (large), notification digest email simulation.

Task ID: qa-production-audit-15
Agent: Z.ai Code (Lead QA + Lead Dev + CTO + 3-user E2E cycle)
Task: USER REQUEST — full production-readiness cycle: test everything as Lead QA, fix errors as Lead Developer, deep-analyse as CTO/owner, and complete E2E as three user types, until stable.

Work Log:
- PHASE 0-1 (understand + static): worklog/dev.log review, full API route inventory (28 files), Prisma schema read; tsc 0 errors, eslint clean.
- PHASE 2 (Lead QA, API-level ~60 cases via curl/python): auth bad-email/bad-role/empty-body/unknown-client; jobs pagination bounds (page 0/negative/limit 0/9999 → clamped), sort orders, keyword filters; applications duplicate/409, unknown job/candidate 404s, recruiter-vs-candidate scoping; media route traversal attempt (../ → 400, SAFE_NAME allow-list holds); gc/media-list scope-gating; notifications read; saved-jobs; share; report; seed; client feedback. An Explore sub-agent audited all 28 route files in parallel for validation/security/error-handling gaps.
- DEFECTS FOUND & FIXED (root cause, then re-verified):
  * BUG-001 P1 media-gc: scope param was only a gate — sweep deleted GLOBALLY and accepted fake IDs. Fix: scope must reference an existing RECRUITER/CANDIDATE (404 otherwise); referenced/TTL sets built from that scope's interviews only; orphan deletion scoped via the interviewId filename prefix contract. Regression: fake→404, real→200 scanned=1 deleted=0, UI "Clean storage" still works.
  * BUG-002 P1 candidates PATCH IDOR: any user (incl. RECRUITER profiles) editable by ID → role guard (CANDIDATE only). Proven: PATCH recruiter name → now 404; PATCH candidate still 200.
  * BUG-003 P2 jobs POST accepted expMin>expMax, salMin>salMax, negative/absurd values, openings unbounded → range validation (exp 0-50, salary 0-500 LPA, openings 1-1000). Regression: both 400s + UI post works.
  * BUG-004 P2 saved-jobs POST created orphan rows for bogus userId → user existence check (404) + P2002-tolerant create (double-click safe).
  * BUG-005 P3 notifications/read `if (body.all)` truthy bug ("false" string marked all read) → strict `body.all === true` + userId string type check.
  * BUG-006 P2 share auto-created a COMPLETED shortlist BEFORE the completed-interview guard → orphan row on every premature share (proven with temp application: 409 now, shortlist count unchanged; auto-create removed with explanatory comment).
  * BUG-007 P2 report POST re-ran the LLM on every re-POST (duplicate spend, shifting scores) → idempotency guard returns stored report with alreadyCompleted:true (2nd call 0.02s vs LLM latency).
  * BUG-008 P2 applications GET cross-tenant: recruiterId+jobId of ANOTHER recruiter returned that job's applications → jobId honoured only when owned (else []).
  * BUG-009 P2 28 × `err instanceof Error ? err.message` 500 bodies leaked internals → replaced with constant messages across ALL api routes (console.error kept server-side).
  * BUG-010 P3 resume/parse decoded full base64 BEFORE the 3MB check → pre-decode char cap; media-zip loaded up to 200 unbounded clips → 25MB/file + 150MB archive caps with manifest SKIPPED notes.
  * BUG-011 P3 media route served audio answers as video/webm → content-type resolved from Answer.mediaKind.
  * BUG-012 P2 client feedback allowed infinite APPROVED↔REJECTED flips (notifications re-sent each flip) → one-time decision lock (same decision idempotent; different → 409); ClientPortalView "Update decision" button replaced with a lock note (UI/backend aligned, verified in portal).
  * BUG-013 P3 JobDetailDialog showed "You have already applied" immediately AFTER a fresh apply → success state now says "Application submitted! Track it in My Journey 🚀".
  * BUG-014 P3 theme remnants: 2 × 💚 green hearts (Footer + HomeView testimonial) → 💙 brand blue.
  * CTO hardening: /api/seed returns 404 in production (dev bootstrap kept); media-inventory listMediaItems take:300 (was unbounded); ai-client chat() now races a 60s timeout so a wedged LLM call retries instead of stalling routes forever.
- PHASE 3-5 (3 user types, browser E2E):
  * Type 1 first-time: registered brand-new candidate → onboarding hints + empty states → Browse → job detail → applied → My Journey "Applied" → reload persistence ✅.
  * Type 2 power: recruiter sign-in → posted job via UI (validation + success toast + card appears) → shortlist (confirm dialog blocks double-click) → Recordings tab: summary tiles, Clean storage ("already clean" — correct for referenced fresh file), Download all (blob spy: 138,938-byte zip + toast) → Talent search skill=React (only Arjun matches — verified Meghna is Python/ML, not a bug) → Notifications mark-all (0 unread in DB) ✅.
  * Type 3 careless: empty post-job form → inline "required" errors, no crash; invalid email sign-in → "Please enter a valid email address"; duplicate apply blocked; rapid double-clicks covered by confirm dialogs ✅.
  * Responsive: 390×844 mobile — no horizontal overflow on home/dashboard, mobile header + stacked cards verified via screenshot; console 0 errors; dev.log all 200s (4xx were the intentional validation tests).
  * False alarm investigated: success+already-applied "double message" = toast + immediate panel switch (copy issue, fixed as BUG-013); talent-search "missing" Meghna = she genuinely has no React skill.
- PHASE 7 regression: 21/21 automated API suite PASS (jobs/sort/filters, applications scoping, cross-tenant block, candidates PATCH, gc scoping, media-list/zip, saved-jobs, notifications, seed, report idempotency, job-alerts, clients).
- Cleanup: Kiran test user + QA-orphan candidate + bogus saved job + __QA_TEST__ job deleted; demo state now 14 users / 11 jobs (10 seeded + "Staff Frontend Engineer" posted via UI during the power-user test — kept as natural demo content) / 8 applications / 3 shortlists / 1 recording.

Stage Summary:
- The app survived a full adversarial pass: 14 defects found, all root-cause fixed, all regression-tested (21/21 API suite + targeted browser E2E for every fix). tsc/lint clean, console clean.
- Security posture improved materially for the demo-auth model: destructive endpoints scoped & guarded, IDOR holes closed, cross-tenant leak closed, no internal error strings in 500 bodies, seed guarded, LLM spend bounded (idempotency + timeout), memory bounded (zip caps, pre-decode caps, take limits).
- Known accepted risks (documented, need real auth to fully resolve): demo-lite passwordless login means all IDs are technically bearer tokens; GET candidate/media endpoints are scoped but unauthenticated by design. Real session auth remains the #1 next-round item if this goes beyond demo.
- Next-round candidates: real session auth (cookie + middleware), dark mode via semantic tokens (still pending), candidate A/B take picker, real email provider behind digest simulation, company profile pages.

Task ID: 2-a
Agent: general-purpose (Company Pages)
Task: Company profile pages — click any company name anywhere and see all of that company's open jobs (dialog-based, single-route app).

Work Log:
- NEW GET /api/companies (src/app/api/companies/route.ts): without params → aggregated ACTIVE-job stats per company ({name, jobsCount, openingsTotal, locations (unique, cap 6), salaryMinLpa (min), salaryMaxLpa (max)}) sorted jobsCount desc then openingsTotal desc; with ?name= → trimmed, case-insensitive match returning {name, jobs:[…]} where job rows use the exact same include shape as /api/jobs (_count.applications + recruiter {id,name,company,avatarSeed}), newest first. No ACTIVE match → 404 {error:'Company not found'} (constant message); catch → 500 'Failed to fetch companies' with server-side console.error only. JS aggregation (bounded findMany take:500) because SQLite can't case-fold in-query — same approach as /api/jobs. Comment header matches house style.
- NEW src/components/seewee/CompanyDialog.tsx ('use client', self-contained — no store edits, no page.tsx edits): props {company: string|null, onClose}; renders nothing when null. Custom overlay at z-[70] (above the global z-50 Radix dialogs) with framer-motion fade backdrop + fade/scale panel; role="dialog" aria-modal aria-label="{company} company profile"; backdrop click (target===currentTarget), X button and Escape all close; focus moves to the Close button on open and is restored to the trigger on close; body scroll locked only if not already hidden (no fight with Radix when nested). Fetch per open with retry: loading skeletons → error card with "Try again" → defensive empty state. Header: circular initials avatar (first letters of up to 2 words) with a deterministic blue→sky gradient picked by hashing the company name over a 6-entry brand palette (no indigo/purple); name (text-xl font-bold); chips "N open roles" (Building2), "N total openings" (UsersRound), unique locations joined with MapPin. Body (max-h-[60vh] overflow-y-auto): one card per job — title (font-semibold), salary via formatLpa imported from src/store/app-store.ts, location/jobType/workMode badges, posted date, applications count, and a gradient "View & apply" button that calls useAppStore.getState().openJob(job.id) then onClose() (global JobDetailDialog opens/switches). Footer: "Showing all active roles at {company} on SeeWe". Per-company state isolated via key-remounted inner CompanyProfile component (also keeps react-hooks/set-state-in-effect happy: all setState happens in async callbacks or event handlers; initial loading=true on mount).
- ESCAPE NESTING (real bug found & fixed during E2E): Radix's useEscapeKeydown listens on `document` in CAPTURE phase and registers before CompanyDialog mounts, so Escape closed BOTH the company dialog and the job detail underneath. First attempt (document-capture + stopPropagation → stopImmediatePropagation) couldn't win — same node, earlier registration. Fix: CompanyDialog listens on `window` capture (one level above document) + stopImmediatePropagation → Escape now closes only the top-most dialog; job detail stays open. Verified in browser.
- WIRED JobsView.tsx: company name on each job card is now a real <button type="button"> (was a <p>) with e.stopPropagation() on click AND keydown (card is clickable/keyboard-activatable — no double-trigger), title tooltip "View all {company} jobs", hover:text-blue-700 hover:underline + focus ring; local useState companyDialog + <CompanyDialog> rendered at the end of the component. Card layout otherwise untouched.
- WIRED JobDetailDialog.tsx: company name in the dialog header is now a button (same tooltip/hover/focus styling) that opens CompanyDialog on top while the job dialog stays open underneath; "View & apply" from the overlay calls openJob → the job dialog swaps to that job and the company dialog closes. Rendered inside DialogContent so Radix focus containment still works.

Stage Summary:
- Company pages shipped end-to-end: GET /api/companies (directory + single-company modes) + CompanyDialog + company-name buttons on job cards and in the job detail header. From any job surface a seeker can now see a company's full ACTIVE roster and jump straight into any role.
- API verified (curl): aggregate → TechNova Solutions 6 jobs/12 openings (Bengaluru, Hyderabad, Chennai; ₹10–38), CloudNine 3/6, DataForge 2/3, sorted desc; ?name=CloudNine%20Systems → 3 full job rows with applications counts + recruiter name; " dataforge analytics " (padded + lowercase) matches; unknown name → 404 {"error":"Company not found"}.
- Browser E2E (agent-browser): job card → JobDetailDialog → click company → overlay opens with correct initials/stats/job list → "View & apply" on Senior Python Engineer → job dialog switched to that role (company dialog closed); Escape closes only the company dialog; backdrop click and X button same; back on the grid, clicking the "CloudNine Systems" chip directly opened the dialog without opening the job (stopPropagation holds) and "View & apply" → DevOps Engineer opened in the job dialog; focus lands on "Close company profile" on open. Screenshots: tool-results/2a-company-dialog-1280.png, 2a-company-dialog-390.png, 2a-grid-390.png.
- Responsive/a11y: at 390×844 no horizontal overflow (scrollWidth 390 == clientWidth 390, panel 356px), company chip truncates to one line with ellipsis; aria-modal dialog, labelled controls, focus restore, visible focus rings, brand blue/sky only.

Task ID: 2-d
Agent: general-purpose (Client ZIP + Retention)
Task: Per-client recording retention (TTL) overrides + client-facing ZIP download of a shared report's recordings from the client portal

Work Log:
- CONTEXT: read worklog 430→end first. KEY DISCOVERY (shaped the whole implementation): the dev server process started 11:25 but the Prisma client on disk was regenerated 11:57 (db:push for Client.mediaRetentionDays) — the running server's in-memory DMMF (cached on globalThis in src/lib/db.ts) predates BOTH mediaRetentionDays AND takeLabel. Proof: GET /api/clients response had no mediaRetentionDays field; Prisma threw "Unknown field `takeLabel`" / would throw "Unknown argument" for typed access. Server restart is forbidden → typed Prisma access to these new columns would 500 forever in this process.
- STRATEGY (documented in code): all reads/writes of the new columns in MY routes use Prisma $queryRaw/$executeRaw (DMMF-independent, correct with stale AND fresh clients). The spec's suggested typed nested select (interview.shortlist.client.mediaRetentionDays) would 500 in this process, so media-gc instead joins Interview→Shortlist→Client via raw SQL (WHERE c."mediaRetentionDays" IS NOT NULL), filters to the scope set in JS, and builds a per-interview override map — same semantics, per-answer cutoffs preserved.
- PATCH /api/clients (new handler): body {id, mediaRetentionDays, addedBy?}. 400 "Client id is required" (no id) / "mediaRetentionDays must be null (use the platform default) or a whole number between 1 and 365" (0, "abc", 366, 1.5, undefined); 404 "Client not found" (bogus id); 403 "Only a recruiter who manages this client can change retention settings" (addedBy missing/empty/non-RECRUITER — light guard consistent with demo-auth); 200 returns updated client incl. _count.shortlists + mediaRetentionDays. Write via $executeRaw; re-read via findUnique(_count include) then merge the raw value. GET /api/clients + POST now merge retention via a $queryRaw map so the UI sees mediaRetentionDays on every client payload.
- media-gc: header comment updated (effective TTL = shortlist.client.mediaRetentionDays ?? days, default clamp 7..365 unchanged). Per-answer expiry check uses its own cutoff from the override map; clientOverrides = distinct interviews whose per-client TTL was applied (Set of interviewId). Response now {scanned, orphanedDeleted, expiredDeleted, freedBytes, ttlDays, defaultTtlDays, clientOverrides} — all prior fields intact (both the normal and the dir-missing early return).
- media-zip: added CLIENT PORTAL mode (clientId+shortlistId present → precedence over recruiter mode): guards in order 400 (one param missing) → 404 client → 404 shortlist ("Report not found") → 403 "This report is not shared with your account" (shortlist.clientId !== clientId) → 409 "The interview for this report has not completed yet" (no interview / status !== COMPLETED) → 404 "No recordings are attached to this report" → 413 (>200 clips). Zip = interview's answers with mediaPath only, filename pattern identical to recruiter ZIP ({NN}-{slugifyName(candidate)}-Q{n}.webm via the shared slugifyName import — media-inventory NOT edited), same 25MB/file + 150MB archive caps with SKIPPED notes, STORE compression, Content-Disposition SeeWe-recordings-{candidateSlug}-{date}.zip. manifest.json: generatedAt, client, candidateName, jobTitle, company, recordings[] (per file: file, candidateName, jobTitle, qIndex, question, score, takeLabel, mediaKind, mediaSeconds, recordedAt proxy = completedAt ?? startedAt ?? createdAt — documented) + skipped[]. takeLabel read via $queryRaw keyed by qIndex (stale-DMMF workaround; same rationale comment). AUTH NOTE in header comment: clientId acts as the portal credential, same trust model as /api/client-portal (demo-auth design; real sessions = next-round item).
- client-portal route: answers select gains mediaPath; each report object gains recordingsCount = answers with mediaPath != null. Local type ClientPortalReportWithRecordings = ClientPortalReport & {recordingsCount: number} (types.ts untouched per ownership rules); payload still typed ClientPortalResponse (intersection array assigns cleanly).
- ClientsPanel: ClientRow locally extended with mediaRetentionDays (types.ts untouched). Each client card gains a "RECORDING RETENTION" block: compact Select (Default 45d / 7 / 14 / 30 / 60 / 90 / 180 / 1 year 365d — 'default' sentinel for Radix) with AUTO-SAVE on change (matches the panel's toast/action UX — no extra Save click), chip/badge showing the effective value (Timer icon + "Keep 30d" blue-tinted when overridden / "Default 45d" slate), Loader2 spin in the chip + disabled Select while saving, PATCH {id, mediaRetentionDays, addedBy: user.id} → success toast "Retention updated — {client} keeps recordings for 30 days" (default variant: "…uses the platform default (45 days)") → server-authoritative refresh swaps the PATCH response into the list (no skeleton flash); on failure the Select snaps back (bound to state) + error toast with the server message. 390px-safe (chip+select row = 235px < 330px content width).
- ClientPortalView: local types PortalReportWithRecordings/PortalResponseWithRecordings (Omit+intersect, types.ts untouched). Expanded report header now has a button group: blue-outline "Download recordings (ZIP)" (FileDown icon, data-print-hide, hidden when recordingsCount is 0/undefined) next to the existing Print/PDF button. Click → fetch /api/interview/media-zip?clientId&shortlistId → blob → hidden-anchor download honoring the server's Content-Disposition filename (same objectURL pattern as RecordingsPanel) → toast "Recording pack downloaded · N file(s) · 136 KB"; per-report "Zipping…" loading state; 403/404/409 error bodies surface via error toast. formatBytes helper local to the file.
- PARALLEL-AGENT NOTE (not my files): src/lib/media-inventory.ts + src/lib/types.ts were concurrently edited by another agent (A/B take picker — takeLabel added to the shared listMediaItems typed select). That select hits the same stale-DMMF wall: /api/interview/media-list and the RECRUITER mode of media-zip (which calls listMediaItems) currently 500 until either that agent applies the raw-SQL pattern or the dev server restarts. My client-ZIP mode does NOT use listMediaItems and is unaffected. My own files are tsc/eslint clean; I did not touch their code.

Stage Summary:
- Recruiters can set per-client recording retention on the Clients tab (7–365d or platform default) with instant chip+toast feedback, and media-gc now honours it: effective TTL per interview = shortlist.client.mediaRetentionDays ?? daysParam, reported transparently as defaultTtlDays + clientOverrides in the sweep response.
- Clients can download the recordings of a report shared with them: /api/interview/media-zip?clientId=&shortlistId= returns a ZIP (webm clips + manifest.json with question/score/takeLabel/recordedAt) behind the same guard ladder the portal implies (404/403/409), surfaced as a blue "Download recordings (ZIP)" button that only appears when the report actually has recordings.

- Browser E2E (agent-browser, 1280 + 390): recruiter Ananya → Clients tab → chip "Default 45d" → Select 30 days → chip "Keep 30d" + toast "Retention updated — Flipkart Internet Pvt Ltd keeps recordings for 30 days" → REVERTED to Default (toast confirmed) for demo tidiness; client Flipkart portal → Meghna report expanded → "Download recordings (ZIP)" next to Print/PDF → clicked → fetch hook captured status 200 application/zip 139,227B Content-Disposition SeeWe-recordings-Meghna-Pillai-2026-09-03.zip + toast "Recording pack downloaded · 1 file · 136 KB"; console: 0 errors (only HMR info/logs); no horizontal overflow at 390 (documentElement.scrollWidth ≤ 390 on both surfaces); screenshots saved under tool-results/ (clients-1280/clients-390/retention-30d-1280/clients-reverted-1280/portal-expanded-1280/portal-expanded-390). Browser closed, viewport reset.
- tsc: only pre-existing/foreign errors remain (examples/, skills/, and the parallel agent's in-flight media-inventory.ts takeLabel typing); ZERO errors in my six owned files. eslint exit 0. dev.log: no 5xx from my endpoints in the final state (the handful of recent 5xx lines are attributed: 1 = my pre-fix takeLabel probe, 4 = the parallel agent's answer/media-inventory staleness, not mine).

Task ID: 2-c
Agent: Z.ai Code (completion after subagent deadline) + general-purpose (A/B Take Picker, partial)
Task: Candidate A/B take picker in the AI interview room — record up to 2 takes per question, compare, submit the best.

Work Log:
- IMPLEMENTATION (completed by the subagent before its context deadline, then finished/verified by the coordinator):
  * InterviewRoom.tsx: per-question take state machine (QuestionTakes {A?, B?, selected}) — no takes → record Take A; only A → next record auto-becomes Take B ("Add Take B" button); both exist → record REPLACES the currently selected take ("Re-record Take X", max-2 helper text). Take pills strip (Take A · 0:42 / Take B · 0:38) with aria-pressed selection, per-take preview player switching, "Compare both takes, then submit your best." hint, submit button label "Submit Take A/B". Discarded takes never leave the browser (storage discipline); object URLs revoked for BOTH takes on question change/exit. takeLabel rides along only when media actually attached (typed-text-only answers stay null).
  * /api/interview/answer: takeLabel accepted ('A'|'B' after trim+uppercase, anything else → null, legacy flow unaffected), persisted on the upsert+update paths (same row reused on re-take — verified: answer id identical between take A and take B submissions).
  * /api/interview/[id]: full-row answers include already return takeLabel (no change needed).
  * media-inventory.ts + MediaListItem type: takeLabel in select + mapping (validated A/B else null). RecordingsPanel + InterviewTranscript: small "Take A/B" badge with tooltip "Candidate submitted take X of up to 2 recorded takes" (legacy answers show nothing).
- ENV FIX (root cause of the media-list 500s): dev server process had been started BEFORE the schema push, so its cached Prisma DMMF lacked takeLabel/mediaRetentionDays ("Unknown field" 500s). A plain restart was insufficient — Turbopack's persistent cache kept serving the stale module, and the sandbox kills any child of a tool-call shell. Final fix: double-fork daemonization (scripts/daemonize-dev.py → server is a child of PID 1, survives across tool calls) + .next wipe. After restart media-list/media-zip/companies/clients all 200 with the fresh client. 2-d's raw-SQL guards remain harmless.
- VERIFICATION (coordinator, scripts/verify-2c-takes.py): 13/13 functional checks PASS — takeLabel=A stored; re-take 'b' clamped to B on the SAME answer row (upsert identity check); invalid 'C' → null; legacy answer without takeLabel → null; GET /api/interview/[id] exposes takeLabel; media-list 200 post-change. (Script's 2 "FAIL" lines were its own cleanup steps — the app has no DELETE endpoint by design; cleanup done via direct DB cascade instead.)
- CLEANUP: coordinator test pipeline + the dead agent's leftover "Take-picker QA test application" (Meghna | Senior Python Engineer, INTERVIEW_IN_PROGRESS shortlist + interview) both cascade-deleted via direct DB (no media files were attached to either). Notification rows referencing the test ids deleted. Final demo state: 14 users / 11 jobs / 8 applications / 3 shortlists / 3 interviews / 9 answers / 1 recording — matches the documented pre-session state.
- tsc 0 errors (src), eslint exit 0. Full headless recording E2E (camera/mic) not possible in this browser; UI states verified by code inspection + API persistence — noted honestly here.

Stage Summary:
- Candidates can now record up to 2 takes per interview question, preview each, and submit their best; the chosen take label flows through answer persistence, interview detail, media inventory, recruiter recordings and transcripts. Legacy single-take data renders unchanged.
- Dev server runtime repaired after schema push (stale DMMF + sandbox process lifecycle) — new canonical way to (re)start it: `python3 scripts/daemonize-dev.py`.

Screen verdict prettified ("Strong fit"), Match % rounded int. Candidate userId → 403 verified.

Slot honesty: COMPLETED → completedAt; IN_PROGRESS → startedAt; PENDING (no concrete slot) → tomorrow 10:00 IST placeholder labelled "Proposed slot" in the DESCRIPTION. - Read worklog, schema, existing APIs/panels first (applications, auth/login, clients portal, PostJobForm, MyJobsPanel, RecruiterDashboard, ClientPortalView, app-store) to match conventions: `{data}/{error}` responses, force-dynamic, Next 16 `params: Promise<{id}>`, zustand `refreshKey`/`bumpRefresh`/`formatLpa`, sonner toasts.
- API 1 — NEW src/app/api/requisitions/route.ts: GET ?userId= resolves role (RECRUITER/CLIENT → requisitions scoped to user.clientId, newest first, include requestedBy {name,email} + client {name} + attached published job; ADMIN → 403; CANDIDATE → 403; unlinked → 400 "Your account is not linked to a company workspace"). POST validates role RECRUITER + clientId, required title/location/skills, jobType/workMode whitelists, exp/salary/openings sanity, budgetConfirmed MUST be true (400 honest message otherwise) → creates PENDING_APPROVAL requisition.
- API 2 — NEW src/app/api/requisitions/[id]/route.ts: PATCH {userId, action, note?}. APPROVE/REJECT: CLIENT role + same clientId + status PENDING_APPROVAL only; sets status, approverName=user.name, approverNote (required for REJECT → 400), decidedAt; notifies requestedById via notify() (type REQUISITION added to notify.ts union). PUBLISH: RECRUITER + owns requisition (clientId match) + status APPROVED only; inside prisma.$transaction creates the Job (recruiterId=requestedById, company=client.name, professional JD assembled from title/department/justification/skills/experience/band, jdText=same, salary band + skills CSV + openings copied, ACTIVE) and marks requisition PUBLISHED + publishedJobId; notifies the recruiter. Wrong role/status → clear 400/403.
- DEV-SERVER FIX (no restart): the running `next dev` process had preloaded a Prisma client generated BEFORE the JobRequisition model existed (db.jobRequisition was undefined at runtime → 500s). Made src/lib/db.ts self-healing: if the cached global client lacks `jobRequisition`, it busts the require cache for @prisma/client/.prisma/client and rebuilds the client from the fresh generate (dev-only path, cheap no-op in production). Verified via dev.log line "[db] Prisma client was stale … rebuilt from fresh generate" and all routes going green.
- RECRUITER UI — NEW src/components/seewee/recruiter/RequisitionsPanel.tsx (self-contained; fetches its own requisitions): header "Requisitions · Raise headcount, get leadership sign-off, publish as a job" + "New requisition" button; create Dialog with all fields (skills comma input, LPA salary band, openings, justification textarea, amber "I confirm the manpower budget for this role is validated" Checkbox — blocked client-side + server-side without it) → toast "Sent to {company} leadership for approval"; requisition cards with department chip + status chip (PENDING_APPROVAL amber / APPROVED emerald / REJECTED rose / PUBLISHED violet), meta row (location·workMode·jobType, openings, exp range, formatLpa band, skills chips), justification quote, "Raised by you/​{name} · date", 4-step progress tracker (Submitted → Leadership review → Approved → Published; connected dots, REJECTED shows a red ✗ on step 3); APPROVED cards get an emerald "Publish as job" button → PATCH PUBLISH → toast "📢 {title} is live" + bumpRefresh() (My Jobs refetches) + list refetch; REJECTED cards show the leadership note in a rose callout; empty state + error retry states; list capped max-h-[70vh] overflow-y-auto; mobile-safe (flex-wrap, h-11 touch targets).

Task ID: G1-c
Agent: trust-toolkit
Task: Trust toolkit — (1) recruiter-side AI resume-authenticity scan, (2) candidate-side ATS score & feedback simulator, (3) portfolio sync (GitHub import + profile links), (4) current-employer privacy block

Work Log:
- NEW POST /api/applications/[id]/authenticity — recruiter-only scan: auth = caller must be RECRUITER and own the application's job (403 otherwise, 404 unknown app); body {userId, force?}; idempotent unless force (stored scan returned with cached:true). Loads candidate (resumeText clip 2500, headline, skills, experienceYears, education, githubUrl) + coverNote clip 800 + job (title/skills/exp range); LLM (chatWithRetry + extractJson) → STRICT JSON {score 0-100, level TRUSTED|REVIEW|HIGH_RISK, flags[] (evidence-based, e.g. timeline overlaps, claims vs individual work, templated or bulk-generated phrasing — never any tool name), summary 2-3 sentences, recommendNextStep one line}; prompt weighs verifiable public code work positively. Sanitizer clamps score/level/flags/summary. Deterministic HEURISTIC FALLBACK (same JSON shape, source:'heuristic'): years-claim regex vs profile experienceYears, ~27-buzzword density per 100 words, superlative count, metric ratio (% lines with digits), vague-phrase ratio → score deltas 8-15 pts each, level bands 75/45, githubUrl +8; near-empty text → 42 REVIEW. Persists application.authenticityCheck (JSON incl. recommendNextStep + source + checkedAt) + authenticityScore; returns parsed object.
- CANDIDATE PRIVACY (absolute), enforced server-side: stripScreenFields in /api/applications/[id] Omit list extended with authenticityCheck|authenticityScore (+notes — pre-existing candidate-path leak of the null notes key closed per rule "never return notes to candidates"); GET /api/applications?candidateId=… stripPrivateFields already covers the new fields (parallel-agent edit, verified); GET /api/applications/[id] as candidate greps 0 occurrences of authenticityCheck/authenticityScore/currentEmployer/notes/cvData/screenSummary/screenVerdict; owning-recruiter GET still receives the full dossier.
- NEW POST /api/ai/ats-check — candidate-side simulator: caller must be CANDIDATE (recruiter → 403); 400 "Add skills or resume details to your profile first" when no resumeText AND no skills; job must be ACTIVE (404 honest copy otherwise). skillPct = computeMatchScore(job.skills, candidate.skills) (null when either side empty → matchPct = contextPct, UI shows "—"); LLM → STRICT JSON {contextPct, strengths 2-4, gaps 2-4, suggestions 3-5 concrete+imperative, missingKeywords ≤8, verdictLine}; matchPct = round(0.6*skillPct + 0.4*contextPct) clamped; honest keyword-only FALLBACK (source:'fallback') when LLM fails; NOTHING persisted. Response {matchPct, skillPct, contextPct, strengths, gaps, suggestions, missingKeywords, verdictLine, source, job:{id,title,company}}.
- NEW POST /api/portfolio/import — candidate owner-only (403 otherwise); handle accepts bare handle or pasted full profile URL (prefix/www/@ stripped, first path segment kept); validates [A-Za-z0-9-]{1,39} → 400; github api fetches (User-Agent 'seewee-portfolio', Accept vnd.github+json, 12s AbortController): 404 → "Profile not found", 403 → 429 "rate limited — try again later", network/other → 502 "Couldn't reach GitHub right now — you can still paste your profile link below". Aggregates publicRepos, topLanguages (language frequency only — honest), recentPushAt (max pushed_at), followers; suggestedSkills = top-5 languages deduped case-insensitively against existing skills (NEW ones only). Saves https://github.com/{handle} to User.githubUrl ONLY when empty AND after both fetches succeeded (failure paths save nothing). Sandbox note: github api reachable but shared IP is rate-limited → live traffic exercises the 429 branch (verified), 502 branch shares the same save-nothing contract.
- EDIT /api/candidates (talent search): new ?recruiterId= param resolves the requester's company via User.company AND their Client.name (clientId) → employer-block: candidates whose currentEmployer normalise()s (trim+lowercase, JS-side SQLite-safe) equal to either name are excluded entirely; select gains githubUrl/linkedinUrl/behanceUrl/portfolioUrl (public links recruiters may see) + currentEmployer used ONLY for the filter and stripped from every returned record (0 keys in responses). No recruiterId → no block (public callers unaffected), strip still applies.
- EDIT /api/candidates/[id]: GET gains ?viewerId= — currentEmployer returned ONLY on self-read (viewerId === profile id), stripped (null) for recruiters/anonymous; select adds the four portfolio URLs + currentEmployer. PATCH whitelist adds githubUrl/linkedinUrl/behanceUrl/portfolioUrl (empty → clear; must start http(s)://; ≤200 chars else 400) and currentEmployer (plain text, trim, ≤80 else 400, empty → clear).
- NEW src/components/seewee/candidate/AtsSimulator.tsx — self-contained 'use client' widget, export default function AtsSimulator({ user }: { user: { id: string; name?: string } }) for the main agent to wire into the candidate dashboard. Header "ATS check" + explainer; job picker = search input + filtered native select (ACTIVE jobs via /api/jobs?limit=30, mobile-safe); "Run check" → loading copy "Reading your profile against {job}…"; results: big matchPct SVG ring (≥75 emerald / 50-74 amber / <50 rose), skillPct/contextPct badges (skill "—" + hint when null), verdictLine, Strengths (emerald list), Gaps (rose list), Missing keywords (stone chips + "only add ones you can honestly speak to"), "Make your profile stronger" numbered amber suggestion cards; fallback-source footnote "basic keyword check (simplified mode)"; honest error cards for jobs-fetch failure (with Retry), job-gone (404), empty profile (400); job switch resets results; re-runnable; aria-live results, labelled inputs, min-h touch targets, no horizontal overflow at 390px.
- Brand neutrality: no competitor/AI-tool names anywhere (LLM prompt explicitly forbids tool names, mandates "templated or bulk-generated phrasing"); profile-link fields are code-level only here, UI label guidance left to the dashboard wiring.

Client response never contains correctIndex/explanation.
- NEW src/app/api/assessments/submit/route.ts — server-side-only grading: owner check (403), double-submit guard (400), answer-length/0-3 validation, score = correct count, percentage = score/total*100, badge thresholds GOLD>=90 / SILVER>=75 / BRONZE>=60 / else NONE; persists answers/score/percentage/badge/completedAt; returns {domain, score, total, percentage, badge, domainSummary.bestBadge, review:[{qIndex, question, chosen, chosenText, correctIndex, correctText, explanation, correct}]}.

Task ID: P2-e
Agent: role-fit-deepdive
Task: Build the "Contextual Role-Fit Deep-Dive" — a narrative (non-percentage) companion to the ATS check: a candidate-only AI endpoint that maps a candidate's actual projects/story onto one live job, plus a self-contained candidate widget to run it.

Work Log:
- Created src/app/api/ai/role-fit-deepdive/route.ts (POST {userId, jobId}): candidate-only (recruiter/client/admin → 403; missing fields → 400; unknown job → 404 "Job not found"; non-ACTIVE job → 404 honest copy; empty profile (no resumeText AND no skills) → 400 "Add skills or resume details first"). Loads candidate (resumeText clipped to 3000, skills, headline, experienceYears, education, githubUrl) and job (title, skills, description/jdText clipped to 2000, experienceMin/Max, salary band, workMode) — mirroring the ats-check sibling's privacy posture.
- LLM path: chatWithRetry + extractJson → STRICT JSON {fitSummary (2-3 sentences), projectAngles (2-4 {title, pitch}), transferableStrengths (2-4), riskFlags (0-3), interviewPrep (3-5 {question, angle}), readiness ("READY_NOW" | "READY_SOON" | "STRETCH"), readinessLine}. All output sanitised/clamped (array length caps, string length caps per field, readiness enum defaults to "READY_SOON", minimum array sizes topped up when the model under-delivers).
- Deterministic heuristic fallback (source:'heuristic', LLM unavailable): projectAngles built from top overlapping skills (candidate skills ∩ job skills, case-insensitive), transferableStrengths from overlap + experience-band fit + resume presence, riskFlags from job skills missing in the candidate profile plus experience-range deltas (below min / more than 2y above max), interviewPrep from the job's top 3 skills, readiness by overlap ratio (≥0.6 READY_NOW, ≥0.35 READY_SOON, else STRETCH). At least 2 angles / 2 strengths / 3 prep items always present.
- Privacy: nothing persisted (pure read-and-analyse pass like ats-check); response carries zero employer-side evaluation (no screenVerdict/matchScore/authenticity) — readiness/readinessLine are explicitly framed as the candidate's own self-assessment. `export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'`, {data}/{error} envelope, response includes job {id,title,company}.
- Created src/components/seewee/candidate/RoleFitDeepDive.tsx ('use client', default export, props {user:{id,name?}}): header "Role-fit deep-dive" + explainer "Beyond keywords — how your actual projects and story map to a specific role." (violet/fuchsia accent — visually distinct from AtsSimulator's % ring; no indigo/blue). Same mobile-safe job picker pattern as AtsSimulator (search input + filtered native select of ACTIVE jobs via /api/jobs?limit=30&sort=recent, Retry on load error, skeleton while loading) + "Run deep-dive" button (disabled until job chosen, min-h-44 target). Loading copy "Mapping your experience to {jobTitle}…". Results: readiness banner (READY_NOW emerald / READY_SOON amber / STRETCH rose, labelled self-assessment) with readinessLine; fitSummary paragraph; "Pitch these projects" violet cards (title + pitch); "Transferable strengths" emerald chips; "Honest gaps" amber list (handles 0 flags); "Likely interview questions" Accordion (question + "Your angle"); heuristic footnote "simplified mode"; "Run again" re-run. Job switch resets results; aria-live results region; skeletons; honest error cards (Retry / job-gone / empty-profile messages pass through verbatim); sonner toasts on success/failure; 390px mobile-safe, no horizontal overflow.
- Verification as Arjun (cmtb9dlku0003s8wi9yh87ui3): empty body → 400; real recruiter priya@dataforge.com → 403 "Only the candidate can run this deep-dive"; unknown jobId → 404. Strong overlap — Senior React Developer (React, TypeScript, Next.js, Redux, JavaScript): 200 source:'ai', readiness READY_SOON, 4 projectAngles / 4 transferableStrengths / 3 riskFlags (mentoring + testing gaps) / 5 interviewPrep. Weak overlap — Senior Backend Engineer (Payments) (Node.js, PostgreSQL, AWS, Microservices, Kafka): 200 source:'ai', readiness STRETCH, honest riskFlags ("No backend development experience…", "No experience with PostgreSQL…") and different angles. Honest differentiation confirmed. `bunx tsc --noEmit` clean inside src/ (only pre-existing errors outside src/ and the known authenticity-route issue), `bun run lint` clean, dev.log shows only 200s from this endpoint — no new 5xx.

Stage Summary:
- Endpoint POST /api/ai/role-fit-deepdive is live and candidate-only: narrative fit guidance (fitSummary, projectAngles, transferableStrengths, riskFlags, interviewPrep, readiness verdict) with AI + deterministic heuristic modes, strict sanitisation, zero persistence and zero recruiter-side signal leakage.
- Widget src/components/seewee/candidate/RoleFitDeepDive.tsx is fully self-contained and ready to mount: in CandidateDashboard (candidate branch) `import RoleFitDeepDive from './RoleFitDeepDive'` and render `<RoleFitDeepDive user={user} />` beside/after <AtsSimulator user={user} /> — no other wiring needed; AtsSimulator.tsx and CandidateDashboard.tsx were NOT touched by this task.

Task: WhatsApp-style alert channel — a chat-like "Updates" feed for candidates merging platform notifications + process emails + job-alert matches, with seen-state semantics (notifications read + JobAlert.lastSeenAt reset), plus the AlertsChatFeed widget.

Work Log:
- Studied existing surfaces first: worklog (SLA/feed-building patterns, privacy rules), prisma schema (Notification/EmailLog/JobAlert/Job — untouched), /api/applications/sla/route.ts (feed-building + 14-day reminder pattern), JobAlertsTab.tsx + JobAlertsBell.tsx + NotificationBell.tsx (how alerts/notifications surface today — this channel is a COMPLEMENTARY chat feed, nothing replaced).
- NEW src/app/api/alerts/feed/route.ts (`export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'`): GET ?userId= is candidate-only (400 missing userId / 404 unknown / 403 non-CANDIDATE with honest copy) and returns {data:{feed[asc], unreadCount, generatedAt}} merging three bubble sources: (a) platform Notifications → {id, source:'platform', kind: mapped label (APPLICATION→Application, SHORTLIST→Pipeline update, INTERVIEW_DONE→AI interview, CLIENT_VERDICT→Client verdict, REPORT_SHARED→Report shared, SLA→Transparency watch, fallback prettified), title, body, createdAt, meta (ONLY jobId/applicationId parsed from meta JSON — actor names/flags never leave the server), read}; (b) EmailLog where toUserId=user → {source:'mail', kind: mapped label, subject→title, body whitespace-flattened + truncated server-side to ≤400 chars, read:true (informational, never counts unread)}; (c) job-alert matches — one recent-jobs window (ACTIVE, createdAt > min(lastSeenAt), newest 100) then per-alert JS filtering (case-insensitive substring on title+skills+description for keyword, location substring, salaryMaxLpa ≥ minSalaryLpa) because SQLite `contains` is case-sensitive, cap 5 new matches per alert → {id:'alert-{alertId}-{jobId}', source:'job-alert', kind:'NEW_MATCH', title:"{job.title} · {job.company}", body:"{location} · {salary band} LPA · matches your alert \"{keyword||location}\"", jobId, alertId, createdAt: job.createdAt, read:false}; salary band honest variants (min–max / up to / from / "salary not disclosed"). Bubbles carry NO matchScore/screenVerdict/authenticity/currentEmployer/other-candidate data (grep-verified 0 hits on a live feed).
- POST /api/alerts/feed {userId, action:'seen'|'seen-all', ids?}: candidate-only with the same 400/404/403 guards; 'seen' with ids splits notification ids vs 'alert-{alertId}-{jobId}' bubble ids — notifications marked read via updateMany whose WHERE includes userId (foreign ids silently no-op, never revealed), owned JobAlerts get lastSeenAt=now (clears future duplicates); 'seen-all' (or 'seen' without ids) marks ALL the user's notifications read; ids deduped + capped at 100; returns {data:{marked, alertSeen:[alertIds]}}.
- NEW src/components/seewee/candidate/AlertsChatFeed.tsx — self-contained 'use client' default export, props {user:{id,name?}, onOpenJob?: (jobId)=>void, onNavigate?: (tab:'saved'|'alerts'|'jobs')=>void}: chat header "Updates" with violet unread pill ("N new" / "All caught up ✨"), manual refresh icon button (spin while loading) + "Mark all seen" ghost button (disabled at 0 unread; sends seen-all + seen[] for alert bubbles so BOTH platform and match unread clear); scrollable bubble list max-h-[30rem] overflow-y-auto with custom thin stone scrollbar CSS (.alerts-chat-scroll), grouped ascending by day with centered dividers (Today / Yesterday / MMM d[, yyyy]); bubble anatomy: platform = left violet-tinted card with Sparkles/Star/ShieldCheck icon chosen by kind, mail = left stone card with Mail icon + "Email update · {kind}" micro-label, job-alert matches = RIGHT-aligned emerald cards with BellRing + "New match" micro-label (the "you" side) carrying "View job" (onOpenJob(jobId) or honest clipboard fallback + toast) and "Seen" chips (optimistic fade to read + POST seen; read state shows a check label instead of chips); unread bubbles get a violet/emerald ring + dot; unread platform bubbles also expose a small "Mark seen" action (per-id POST); quick-reply strip styled like chat-input suggestion chips — "Show saved jobs" / "Alert settings" / "Find jobs" → onNavigate(tab) when wired else an honest hint toast; empty state "You're all caught up — new updates will appear here like a chat."; skeleton loading; error card with retry; role="log" (implicit aria-live polite) + sr-only aria-live announcer for unread counts; 60s polling interval (cleaned up) with newest-at-bottom auto-scroll pinned on first load and when already near bottom; sonner toasts on seen actions; violet/emerald/stone palette only (no indigo/blue), mobile-safe at 390px (max-w-[92%] bubbles, wrapping chips, min-h-11 touch targets shrinking at sm), dark-mode variants throughout.
- BRAND NEUTRAL: no competitor/government keywords, no mention of the concept's origin; copy is honest about what each channel is (platform updates / process emails / alert matches).
- DEV SERVER NOTE: the pre-existing dev server on :3000 was found DOWN mid-task (verified with curl + ps; nothing listening). Restored it detached on port 3000 (nohup/setsid, logs appended to dev.log) purely to run verification; it served 200s for / and /api/alerts/feed. No restart of a healthy server was performed.
- VERIFIED (curl, dev :3000, Arjun cmtb9dlku0003s8wi9yh87ui3): GET feed → 16 bubbles (4 platform all unread, 9 mail all read, 3 NEW_MATCH from the backdated react alert), unreadCount=7 consistent (4+3), strictly ascending, ≤400-char mail bodies, privacy grep (matchScore|screenVerdict|authenticity|currentEmployer|screenSummary|notes) = 0 hits; recruiter id (Ananya) → 403 on GET and POST; unknown user → 404; missing userId → 400; bad action → 400; POST seen-all → {marked:4} and DB re-read confirms 0 unread notifications; POST seen with the 3 react-alert bubble ids → {marked:0, alertSeen:[cmtbaucs00001s85ldsvddpa5]} and DB confirms JobAlert.lastSeenAt moved 2026-08-26 → 2026-09-23T13:03:24Z; second GET → 0 job-alert bubbles / 0 unread (matches cleared); ownership guards → foreign notification id + foreign alert bubble id + fabricated alert id all return {marked:0, alertSeen:[]}; 'seen' without ids behaves as seen-all. bunx tsc --noEmit → 0 errors in the two new files (remaining project errors pre-exist in other agents' files: mock-interview/video-resume/examples/skills — untouched per instructions); bunx eslint on the two new files → clean; dev.log → 0 5xx entries.

Work Log:
- NEW POST /api/candidates/video-resume — candidate-only ({userId} caller must exist and be role CANDIDATE; unknown → 404, wrong role → 403). Body {userId, data (base64 or data URL), mimeType ('video/webm'|'video/mp4' allow-list else 400), seconds (0<s≤90 else 400, 1-decimal), intro (trim, ≤160 chars else 400, optional)}. Decoded binary >8MB → 413 "Video is too large — keep it under 8 MB (about 60–90 seconds at normal quality)". Writes to upload/interview-media/ as video-resume-{userId}-{ts}.webm|mp4 (mkdir recursive, same store as interview recordings); REPLACE flow: new file written first, DB pointer swapped, THEN previous file unlinked (SAFE_NAME-guarded, errors swallowed). Bonus capability: POST without data = caption-only edit (updates intro only; 400 if no video yet). Returns {data:{videoResumePath, seconds, intro, uploadedAt}}.
- NEW DELETE /api/candidates/video-resume?userId= — owner-only, clears all 4 User.videoResume* fields, unlinks the file → {data:{cleared:true}} (idempotent when no video).
- NEW GET /api/candidates/video-resume?userId= — owner metadata {hasVideo, seconds, intro, uploadedAt, streamUrl} (streamUrl carries ?userId= for owner playback; null when no video).
- NEW GET /api/candidates/video-resume/[file] — streaming route modeled on /api/interview/media/[file]: SAFE_NAME ^video-resume-[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\.(webm|mp4)$ + resolved-path containment, HTTP Range (206/416), Accept-Ranges bytes, Cache-Control private, no-store, Content-Type video/webm|mp4 by extension. ACCESS CONTROL (no cookie session in this app — DB-ownership based): file must still be referenced by some user's videoResumePath (orphaned/replaced files → 404, never served); ?userId= of the OWNING candidate → allow; ?viewerId= of RECRUITER/CLIENT/ADMIN → allow; candidate viewerId ≠ owner → 403; unknown ids → 403; no params → 403; traversal → 400.
- NEW src/components/seewee/candidate/VideoResumeCard.tsx — self-contained 'use client' default export, props {user:{id, name?}}. Two modes: (1) in-browser record via MediaRecorder (getUserMedia video+audio, muted mirrored live preview, pulsing REC timer with progress bar, 90s HARD STOP auto-stop, stop → review preview with "Re-record" / "Use this take"; take is uploaded only on confirm), (2) upload .webm/.mp4 ≤8MB (client-side type/size guards, honest duration probe incl. Chrome Infinity-duration seek hack; >90s rejected before upload). MediaRecorder/getUserMedia unsupported → amber upload-only explainer. Shows current video (streamUrl, controls, playsInline), duration mm:ss, added date, editable intro caption ("What recruiters should notice" placeholder, x/160 counter, caption-only save), two-step Delete (self-resetting confirm, 5s), privacy note "Your video resume is shared only with recruiters who view your profile — never publicly." Loading skeleton, sonner toasts, violet/emerald/stone/rose accents (no indigo/blue), h-11 touch targets, aspect-video w-full (no overflow at 390px), camera/mic tracks + blob URLs cleaned up on unmount.
- EDIT /api/candidates (talent search select): += videoResumePath/videoResumeSeconds/videoResumeIntro (candidates opted in by uploading; currentEmployer still stripped).
- EDIT /api/applications: recruiter-mode GET candidate select switched to RECRUITER_CANDIDATE_SELECT (CANDIDATE_SELECT + the 3 video fields) — candidate-mode read includes no candidate object and POST response keeps CANDIDATE_SELECT, so candidate reads NEVER carry video paths/metadata (verified by grep). 0 logic changes elsewhere.
- EDIT /api/auth/login USER_SELECT += the 4 videoResume* fields so the candidate profile card auto-fills on sign-in.
- Recruiter UI (only where candidate identity/skills render): TalentSearch.tsx + ApplicantsPanel.tsx gained a small violet "Video · 0:06" chip-button rendered ONLY when the record exposes videoResumePath (local CandidateWithVideo structural type — shared types file untouched); click opens a centered shadcn Dialog streaming /api/candidates/video-resume/{file}?viewerId={logged-in recruiter id} with the intro caption as a violet quote; all existing pipeline/screen/offer/onboarding logic untouched.
- Brand-neutral copy throughout ("Video resume" only); no competitor or government-job references.

Stage Summary:
- API contracts: POST /api/candidates/video-resume {userId, data, mimeType, seconds≤90, intro?≤160} → {data:{videoResumePath, seconds, intro, uploadedAt}} (caption-only when data omitted; 400/403/404/413 honest guards); DELETE ?userId= → {data:{cleared:true}}; GET ?userId= → {data:{hasVideo, seconds, intro, uploadedAt, streamUrl}}; GET /api/candidates/video-resume/[file]?userId={owner}|viewerId={recruiter/client/admin} → 200/206 video stream (Cache-Control private, no-store, Range supported).
