Task ID: P2-b
Agent: mock-interview
Task: AI Mock-Interview Practice Mode — candidate-private practice sessions (start/resume/submit/grading/report) + MockInterviewPractice widget for the candidate dashboard

Work Log:
- NEW src/app/api/ai/mock-interview/route.ts — GET ?userId=&history=1 returns {data:{inProgress|null, history (last 6 COMPLETED: id/track/difficulty/overallScore/completedAt/focusAreas from report JSON), stats:{totalSessions, avgScore, bestTrack (highest-avg track, tie→alphabetical)}}}; candidate-only (role gate → 403 for recruiters/clients/anonymous-noncandidate). POST {userId, track, difficulty} — track must be one of 6 fixed tracks (React & Frontend / Node & Backend / Data & SQL / System Design / Behavioral / HR / Case & Product → 400 otherwise), difficulty EASY|MEDIUM|HARD (default MEDIUM, bad value → 400); an IN_PROGRESS session for the user is RESUMED (resumed:true, same mockId, original track/difficulty kept — new track requests don't hijack the open session); else 3 questions generated via chatWithRetry+extractJson with strict sanitising (exactly 3, question 10-400 chars, focusArea ≥3 chars, tip clamped ≤200 chars, server-side qIndex re-indexing) and AI failure falls back to a hand-written bank (6 questions × 6 tracks = 36, tagged EASY/MEDIUM/HARD ×2 each; deterministic pick = 2 matching-difficulty + 1 bridge: EASY→first MEDIUM, MEDIUM→first EASY, HARD→first MEDIUM). Persists MockInterview row (IN_PROGRESS), returns {data:{mockId, track, difficulty, resumed, questions:[{qIndex,question,focusArea,tip}]}}. Tips are deliberately candidate-facing BEFORE answering — practice mode, unlike the real AI interview.
- NEW src/app/api/ai/mock-interview/submit/route.ts — POST {userId, mockId, answers: string[3]}: 400 on bad shapes (non-string answers, wrong count, <10-char answers after trim), 404 unknown mockId, owner-only (403 "belongs to another user"), double-submit guard → 400 "This practice session has already been completed". Per-question grading: 3 parallel chatWithRetry calls → {score 0-100 clamped, feedback (≥15 chars, clamped 600), sampleAnswer (≥25 chars, clamped 900)}; per-question failure → deterministic heuristic fallback (word-count bands 25/40/55/62/68 + digits +5 + connectors +3ea + question-keyword coverage ≤+8 + STAR groups +6/+3 for Behavioral / HR, honest floor 8 for near-empty, capped 5-80 with "Offline review (simplified mode)" copy and honest scaffold sampleAnswer). Overall: one more chatWithRetry → {overallScore clamp 0-100, strengths 2-4, focusAreas 2-4 (≤60 chars each, deduped), summary 2-3 sentences}; AI overall failure → heuristicOverall (avg of per-question, best/worst focusArea-derived strengths/focus areas, honest band copy). Persists report JSON {…, source:'ai'|'heuristic', completedAt} + status COMPLETED + completedAt; returns {data:{overallScore, strengths, focusAreas, summary, perQuestion:[{qIndex,score,feedback,sampleAnswer}], source}}.
- PRIVACY (absolute, server-enforced): both routes + GET are candidate-only with explicit code comments stating practice answers/scores/reports are NEVER surfaced to recruiters or clients and no recruiter-facing endpoint reads MockInterview; owner-only submit; role gate 403 on every path. This is the candidate's own practice score, clearly labelled "private to you" in the UI.
- NEW src/components/seewee/candidate/MockInterviewPractice.tsx — self-contained 'use client' default export, props {user:{id:string; name?:string}}. Header "Practice interview" + emerald ShieldCheck chip "Only you see your practice results — recruiters never do". Setup: 6 track cards (icon + one-line blurb, violet ring selection) + EASY/MEDIUM/HARD segmented control + Start practice (disabled until track chosen); violet resume banner when GET returns inProgress ("questions are kept; typed answers from a previous visit aren't saved" — honest). Quiz: one question at a time, amber "How to approach this" tip box BEFORE answering, min-h-[200px] mobile-safe textarea, progress bar + "2/3" + answered count, Back/Next, Submit-for-feedback enabled only when all 3 answers ≥10 chars, Exit keeps the session IN_PROGRESS server-side (resumable). Report: aria-live region, score ring (emerald ≥75 / amber 50-74 / rose <50), "private to you" chip, summary, strengths (emerald) + focus areas (amber) cards, per-question Accordion (max-h-96 scroll) with score chip + feedback + "A strong answer would sound like" sample-answer box, honest "Simplified offline feedback" footnote when source=heuristic, Practice again button. History strip: compact chips (track · score · date, score-coloured) in max-h-32 scroll + lifetime stats line (N completed · avg · strongest track). Skeleton loading, honest error card with Retry, sonner toasts, min-h-11 touch targets, no indigo/blue (violet/emerald/amber/stone only).
- NOT touched: schema (MockInterview was already applied), CandidateDashboard.tsx, any shared file — widget awaits main-agent wiring (<MockInterviewPractice user={user} />).
- VERIFIED (curl, dev :3000): POST start React & Frontend MEDIUM → 3 AI questions (tips 107-110 chars, mockId cmue45cww0001n2dmy08fbolp); GET history → inProgress shown, history 0, stats zeroed; POST start with a DIFFERENT track → resumed:true, same mockId, original track kept; submit with 3 realistic 100-200-word answers → source:'ai', per-question 85/85/85, overall 85, strengths 3, focusAreas 2, summary + sampleAnswers all populated; double submit → 400 "already completed"; submit as Sneha → 403; GET + POST as recruiter Ananya → 403; POST as client (Flipkart) → 403; invalid track → 400; invalid difficulty → 400; missing userId → 400; non-string answers → 400. dev.log: only 200/400/403 from my calls, 0 5xx (one logged AI-validation retry inside chatWithRetry, recovered to a 200 AI report). bunx tsc --noEmit → 0 errors in my files (pre-existing errors outside src/ and in VideoResumeCard.tsx / video-resume route are NOT mine, untouched); bunx eslint on my files → clean.
- DEMO STATE: exactly ONE MockInterview row in db — Arjun Mehta (cmtb9dlku0003s8wi9yh87ui3), Behavioral / HR, MEDIUM, COMPLETED, overallScore 68, coherent 3-question report (72/74/58 incl. one honest off-topic low scorer so the accordion shows an amber/rose case), completedAt now. All other test rows deleted (deleteMany ran after verification). Widget not mounted anywhere yet — main agent wires it.

- API contracts: GET /api/ai/mock-interview?userId=&history=1 → {data:{inProgress:{mockId,track,difficulty,startedAt,questions[3]}|null, history[≤6]:{id,track,difficulty,overallScore,completedAt,focusAreas}, stats:{totalSessions,avgScore,bestTrack}}} (candidate-only 403 otherwise); POST /api/ai/mock-interview {userId,track,difficulty?} → {data:{mockId,track,difficulty,resumed,questions[3]:{qIndex,question,focusArea,tip≤200}}} (400 invalid track/difficulty; resumes any IN_PROGRESS session first; static bank fallback); POST /api/ai/mock-interview/submit {userId,mockId,answers:string[3]} → {data:{overallScore,strengths[2-4],focusAreas[2-4],summary,perQuestion[3]:{qIndex,score,feedback,sampleAnswer},source:'ai'|'heuristic'}} (owner-only 403, double-submit 400, <10-char answers 400, heuristic fallback always available).

Work Log:
- RATE LIMIT (src/lib/auth.ts): in-memory Map<emailLower,{fails,lockedUntil}> persisted across hot reloads via globalThis.__seeweeLoginFails singleton; registerLoginFail / isLoginLocked / clearLoginFails — 5 consecutive failures → 15-min lock (lock window runs out, doesn't extend). Login route consults the lock BEFORE password verify (constant-time-safe ordering) → 429 { error: 'Too many failed attempts — try again in N minutes' }.
- DISABLED ACCOUNTS (/api/auth/login): user.disabled now in USER_SELECT; checked FIRST → 403 { error: 'This account has been disabled. Contact support.' } before any password work on the candidate-password path; same 403 applied consistently to ALL paths that resolve/issue users by email — CLIENT registry path, RECRUITER find-or-create, ADMIN demo path, and the demo-lite existing-user path (rule: disabled blocks ALL logins for that email; admin re-enables). registerLoginFail on BAD_CREDENTIALS, clearLoginFails on success; EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED/NO_ACCOUNT behaviors untouched. /api/auth/verify-phone (issues a session) also 403s disabled users.
- ToS CONSENT (/api/auth/register): accepts optional { acceptedTos?: boolean } — when true stamps User.tosAcceptedAt + creates ConsentRecord { kind: 'TOS', version: '1.0' } (both create-path and unverified-re-register path via recordTosConsent $transaction). Backward compatible (field omitted → nothing recorded).

All admin APIs copy the /api/admin/stats demo-auth guard (adminId verified against ADMIN role, force-dynamic, {data}/{error}).

Recruiter-only rows (AI_SCREEN/ATS_PUSH/AI_INVITE/NOTE/CLIENT_DECISION) never reach candidates.

- NEW src/lib/boolean-search.ts — parseBooleanQuery: quote-aware tokenizer (≤24 tokens, ≤60-char terms), case-insensitive AND/OR/NOT, bare leading terms → should (OR across fields), malformed (dangling/adjacent operators, operator-only, pure-NOT, >400 chars) → null = legacy substring behaviour. matchesBooleanQuery(query, lower-cased fields[]): must all present, ≥1 should, no not.
- /api/candidates: accepts `q` (boolean-aware; wins) or legacy `keyword` (unchanged contract) → parseBooleanQuery non-null = JS-side boolean match over headline/name/skills/resumeText/location; null = exact legacy substring path. Zero changes to employer-block/visibility/filters.

Task ID: whitebox-scrollfix-1
Agent: Z.ai Code (main orchestrator)
Task: USER — "ye white screen abhi bhi a arhi hai post krne samay logo dal rhe hai us samay" — the white screen STILL appears at the exact moment of adding the logo in the Post a Job dialog (screenshot: dialog shell rendered, zero content).

Work Log:
- ROOT CAUSE FOUND (third and FINAL layer of this recurring bug): the DialogContent shells carry `overflow-hidden` — which still leaves them as SCROLL CONTAINERS for Chrome's focus-scroll. When the OS file picker opens from the logo <input type="file">, Chrome scrolls EVERY scrollable ancestor to reveal that input, INCLUDING the outer overflow-hidden dialog shell — measured live: shell.scrollTop jumped to 986px (scrollHeight 1528 vs clientHeight 526) into the EMPTY region below the form. The dialog was never crashed/closed — it was showing blank space below its own content = the "white box". Explains why DOM/console always looked healthy, why reload "fixed" it, and why programmatic uploads never reproduced it (no real chooser = no focus-scroll).
- Repro protocol that finally caught it: real label click (not setInputFiles) on "Upload logo" inside the tall form with branding expanded → white box 100% reproducible; DOM dump showed dialog-header at y=-969 while shell rect was centered → scroll, not paint.
- FIX (defence in depth, 4 files):
  (a) NEW src/lib/dialog-scroll-guard.ts — clampDialogShellScroll onScroll handler (snaps shell back to 0,0 the instant anything tries to scroll it) + full root-cause doc.
  (b) RecruiterDashboard.tsx post-job dialog + (c) MyJobsPanel.tsx edit-job dialog: `overflow-hidden` → `overflow-clip` (clip = NOT a scroll container; Chrome cannot programmatic-scroll it) + onScroll={clampDialogShellScroll}.
  (d) Same treatment for JobDetailDialog.tsx + LegalDialog.tsx (identical overflow-hidden shell pattern, same latent risk).
  (e) PostJobForm.pickLogo: after the chooser closes, direct clamp of every [data-slot=dialog-content] shell (belt-and-braces for engines without overflow-clip) before the success toast.
- E2E with fix (agent-browser, real-click protocol): real click on Upload logo label → shell scrollTop stays 0, dialog fully painted (was white box 100% before) → uploaded 11MB 4032x3024 JPEG → processed to 512x384 logo, 2 preview <img>s render, toast path clean, draft autosaved WITH logo (83,680 chars in localStorage) → inner form scroller still scrolls freely (scrollTop 1177 to branding section, shell unaffected). Note: dev server died mid-session (restart required, back on :3000); test draft + test image cleaned up.
- bun lint: only pre-existing scripts/render-candidate-journey.js errors. dev.log: zero errors.

Stage Summary:
- The "white screen while adding logo" family is now CLOSED at its true root: the dialog shell can no longer be scrolled by browser focus-scroll (overflow-clip) and self-heals via the onScroll clamp even if anything else tries. Applied to all 4 non-scrolling dialog shells app-wide.
- Prior layers (from earlier rounds) remain in force: no outside/focus dismissal on file-picker handoff; draft auto-save incl. logo restores everything if anything ever closes anyway.
- webDevReview cron continues monitoring.

Task ID: white-screen-logo-fix-v3
Agent: main (SeeWe maintenance)
Task: Recruiter "white dialog when adding logo in Post a Job" — reported twice, still reproducing after the scroll-guard fix

Work Log:
- Reproduced & investigated with agent-browser (signed in as ananya@technova.com demo): small PNG and 8.6MB BMP both processed fine headless — no crash, no white dialog. Conclusion: the white screen is an environment-specific interaction around the native file-picker handoff (focus restore + scroll-into-view of the `sr-only` input) and/or main-thread freeze during canvas encode on the user's device; also noted body{overflow:hidden}+html{overflow:visible} makes focus-scroll paths unpredictable. In dev mode a React render crash shows the red Next.js overlay, NOT white — so a pure-white dialog points at paint/focus artifacts rather than a render crash.
- HARDENED src/lib/image.ts: decode now downscales INSIDE the decoder via createImageBitmap(file,{resizeWidth:512,resizeQuality:'medium'}) (12MP photos never materialize as full bitmaps; two-stage fallback), added explicit yield-to-paint between decode and the synchronous canvas encode, added LOGO_INPUT_MAX_BYTES=25MB export, guaranteed never-throw contract (resolve null on any failure).
- PostJobForm.tsx logo input is now focus-immune: className `pointer-events-none fixed top-0 left-0 size-px opacity-0` + tabIndex={-1} + disabled={logoBusy} — a fixed element has no scrollable ancestor inside the dialog, so Chrome's scroll-into-view on picker-close can never park a dialog into blank space (this was the click-time white trigger). Removed the old scrollTop patch hack from pickLogo. Added logoBusy state: spinner in the preview box, "Processing…" label, aria-busy — no more perceived freeze.
- RecruiterProfile.tsx: replaced the naive FileReader+300KB-cap pickLogo with the shared processLogoFile pipeline (any size, HEIC-safe, same guarantees), same focus-immune input + spinner, removed now-unused LOGO_MAX_BYTES const, fixed useState-after-early-return lint error.
- NEW src/components/seewee/FormErrorBoundary.tsx: local render boundary wrapped around <PostJobForm> in BOTH RecruiterDashboard (resetKey=String(postOpen)) and MyJobsPanel (resetKey=editingJob.id) — even an unknown future render crash shows a recoverable "Restore my form" panel instead of a white void; remount auto-restores the localStorage draft.
- NEW crash telemetry: src/lib/report-error.ts (window error + unhandledrejection + React-root-emptied watchdog via MutationObserver, sendBeacon to /api/client-log) wired at the top of src/app/page.tsx; NEW src/app/api/client-log/route.ts console.errors `[client-crash:<kind>] message @ url` + stack into dev.log — the next occurrence on the user's device is now fully diagnosable.
- Fixed pre-existing lint errors in scripts/render-candidate-journey.js via eslint-disable header (standalone CommonJS script); lint is green.
- Verified with agent-browser end-to-end: Post-a-Job dialog uploads (small PNG + 8.6MB BMP) → 512px logo renders, "Logo ready (64 KB)" toast, dialog never blanks; My Profile uploader same; /api/client-log telemetry round-trip confirmed in dev.log; bun lint clean. Dev server restarted after it died mid-verify (EADDRINUSE stale process; fresh bun run dev on :3000).

## Task ID: advanced-talent-sourcing-v1 (2026-10-29)

**User ask (Hinglish → full spec):** "Talent search or hiring hub ek jaisa kyu hai… candidate search database hai CV ka har trah ke candidates ka… usme boolean search, normal search, skills search, industry search" + a 16-section production spec for a advanced candidate sourcing module. Talent Search must be a SEPARATE experience from the Hiring Hub (Interview Hub), upgraded into a real sourcing engine.

### 1. Features implemented (all verified live in agent-browser)
- **Engine** (`GET /api/candidates` v2): DB-level prefilter (numeric/date/privacy) → bounded pool (≤1200, never the whole DB to the client) → precise JS matching (SQLite has no CI contains) → **server-side pagination** (page/pageSize/pages) → facets (locations/industries/functionalAreas/notice/experience buckets) → relevance & 8 sort modes. Legacy params preserved.
- **Boolean language** (`src/lib/advanced-search.ts`): real recursive-descent parser — AND/OR/NOT (case-insensitive), `()` nesting to depth 8, `"exact phrases"`, implicit AND; **strict validation** with positioned human messages (unbalanced paren/quote, dangling operator, consecutive ops, empty phrase, limits) — NEVER silent fallback; readable interpreted summary; token-exact skill matching with synonym map (Java ≠ JavaScript; SDE↔Software Engineer); contact masking helpers.
- **16 structured filters**: mandatory/preferred/excluded skills, designation (w/ current-title match), current & previous employer, industry, functional area, employment type, locations (also matches preferred/desired), education, institute, exp range, expected+current CTC range, notice max, languages, last-active freshness, profile-updated-within, search-within-results (`within`).
- **Privacy layer (server-enforced)**: role gate (`resolveRecruiter`) — talent search is recruiter-only; employer-block shield kept; `currentEmployer` never leaves the server; **email/phone masked** in every list/full-profile payload; free-text resume scrubbed of emails/phones (search + preview + API [id]); `cvData/photoData` never selected; anonymous callers get ONLY an aggregate count (landing-page stat fix: "Candidates 156").
- **Contact reveal** (`POST /api/candidates/[id]/contact`): quota'd (60/day) + logged (UsageLog) + employer-block re-check; UI reveal buttons in preview + RecruiterFullProfile.
- **Sourcing tracker** (`/api/sourcing`): 11-status workflow (New→Reviewed→Shortlisted→Contacted→Responded→Interested/Not→Submitted→Interviewing→Hired/On-hold), note/tags/assignment/last-contacted/follow-up, **immutable history trail** (parsed on read — fixed Invalid-Date bug), bulk upsert.
- **Saved searches + alerts** (`/api/saved-searches` + `[id]`): create/rename/duplicate/delete/run; run executes the stored filter set through the same engine, computes **newCount since last run**, enforces quota, per-search alert frequency OFF/DAILY/WEEKLY.
- **Match engine** (`/api/sourcing/match`): transparent rules-based scoring (skills 50/exp 25/loc 10/salary 10/notice 5; unknown = excluded+renormalised, never a failure) with per-component evidence + missing-mandatory list; **optional AI rationale** via z-ai SDK using ONLY verified facts, labelled `rules+AI` vs `rules-only`, advisory-only.
- **CSV export** (`/api/sourcing/export`): role-gated, quota'd (10/day, 300 rows), contacts stay masked by policy, audited.
- **Quotas/usage** (`/api/talent/usage`, `src/lib/talent-config.ts` + client-safe `talent-constants.ts`): 7 quota kinds enforced server-side on every counted op; live usage popover with progress bars.
- **Filter metadata** (`/api/talent/meta`, `/api/talent/teammates`): dropdowns fed by actual stored data; teammates for assignment.
- **UI rebuild** (`TalentSearch.tsx` + `talent/` components): standalone top-level tab (already separate from Interview Hub) — big keyword bar, guided Boolean builder (MUST/ANY/NOT auto-compiles + auto-runs; raw mode with live validation + interpreted summary + 4 example queries incl. the user's maintenance classic), 12-section filter rail with data-backed facets + quick buckets, active-filter chips, search-within, sort, compact/detailed cards, pagination, bulk select (folder/status/export), preview dialog (Profile/Sourcing/Match tabs), synthetic-data lineage banner, empty/loading/error/syntax-error states, dark mode, mobile stacking.

### 2. Files & tables changed
- **Schema (migrated via `bun run db:push`)**: `User` +7 fields (functionalArea, industry, employmentTypePref, preferredLocations, languages, lastActiveAt, profileSource); NEW `SavedSearch`, `SourcingEntry`, `UsageLog`.
- **New libs**: `src/lib/advanced-search.ts`, `src/lib/talent-config.ts`, `src/lib/talent-constants.ts`.
- **New APIs**: `candidates/[id]/contact`, `talent/meta|usage|teammates`, `saved-searches` + `[id]`, `sourcing` (+`export`, `match`).
- **Rewritten**: `api/candidates/route.ts`, `api/candidates/[id]/route.ts` (masking+scrub+PROFILE_VIEW logging), `TalentSearch.tsx`.
- **New UI**: `components/seewee/recruiter/talent/{advanced-types,BooleanPanel,FilterRail,CandidateCard,CandidatePreview,SavedSearches}.tsx`.
- **Patched**: `RecruiterFullProfile.tsx` (contact reveal), `HomeView.tsx` (count stat), `scripts/seed-talent-db.ts`.

### 3. Data / migrations / env
- Migration: none destructive — `prisma db push` additive. Deployment: `bun run db:push` then optionally `bun scripts/seed-talent-db.ts` (idempotent, wipes+recreates only `profileSource=SYNTHETIC_SEED` rows).
- **150 synthetic candidates** across 8 industries with structured work history (482 rows), education (160), certifications (171), staggered freshness — every profile self-labelled `SYNTHETIC_SEED` + `@synthetic.seewee.test` + resume footer disclaimer; passwords null (cannot log in). 2 demo saved searches seeded for ananya@technova.com.
- **Env vars required: NONE new** (AI match uses existing z-ai SDK config; no secrets in client code).

### 4. Integrations needing configuration
- SMTP/SES for real outreach mail (currently in-app outbox pattern only); OCR for scanned PDFs (not attempted, labelled); Postgres/FTS recommended beyond ~5k candidates (bounded-pool search documented).

### 5. Tests executed & results
- Parser unit checks (bun): user's exact advanced example ✓; 8 malformed queries → precise errors ✓; implicit AND ✓; Java≠JavaScript ✓; masking ✓.
- API smoke (curl): boolean search 7 hits + facets ✓; syntax-error 400 w/ message ✓; meta/saved/sourcing/contact/match/run/export ✓ (fixed Prisma select+include error, missing role select, sourcing history parse).
- Browser E2E (agent-browser, ananya@technova.com): 150 results ✓; boolean 7/4/13-hit flows ✓; guided builder auto-compile+auto-run ✓ (fixed double-quoting); industry auto-apply 150→14 ✓; pagination 13 pages ✓; preview 3 tabs ✓; contact reveal (logged) ✓; sourcing status+note+history ✓ (fixed raw-JSON history bug); match 37/100 rules+AI with factual rationale ✓; saved-search run ✓; bulk select 2 → new folder "Demo Pool" ✓; CSV export toast ✓; usage popover 20/500 searches etc ✓; landing stat 156 ✓ (fixed 403); console clean after key-dedupe fix ✓; dev.log: 0 failures/500s; `bun run lint` green; dark mode + 390px mobile verified.

### 6. Known limitations
- SQLite → bounded pool (≤1200) server-side matching; facets computed per request; no FTS index (documented upgrade path).
- Alert "delivery" = in-app newCount on run/open (no background scheduler in stack); weekly/daily gating stored on lastNotifiedAt.
- Outreach email sending intentionally out of scope here (no provider configured) — contact reveal + logging + quotas are the implemented primitives.
- Resume upload/parsing already existed (`/api/resume/parse`, CV pipeline, resume versions) — reused, not re-built.
- Synthetic profiles are clearly labelled in data + UI; production cutover = connect real signup database (same schema).

### 7. Manual QA checklist (recruiter workflow)
1. Login ananya@technova.com → header "Talent Search" → tab loads with 150 candidates + demo banner.
2. Paste the maintenance boolean example → Search → 7 hits, interpreted summary shown.
3. Guided builder: MUST tpm, ANY oee/"sap pm", NOT intern → auto-compiles & auto-runs (4 hits).
4. Filter rail: Industry=Manufacturing auto-applies; chips update; Clear all resets.
5. Search within results: keyword → narrowed set with badge; exit restores.
6. View profile → Reveal contact (logged, quota++) → Sourcing tab: status/note/tags/follow-up/assignee → Save → history trail.
7. Match vs job → pick job → Compute → score + components + AI rationale (advisory).
8. Saved searches → Save current (Daily) → Run → toast with newCount; rename/duplicate/delete.
9. Bulk select → 2 cards → Save to folder (create "Demo Pool") → Export CSV (masked contacts).
10. Usage popover → counters move; syntax-error query "react AND" → inline error, no crash.

## Task ID: talent-search-distinct-v2 (2026-10-29)

**User ask (Hinglish):** "hiring hub or talent search ek jaisa kyu hai — talent search me sirf search features honge; filter options chup ja rahe hain, sab dikhao; view profile dialog bada karo; usme similar-type candidates option chahiye; aur match vs job me recruiter apna JD paste kar sake (ya posted job se fetch ho) — role/responsibility/skill match nahi to experience/location se compensate NAHI hoga, optional filters sirf flag karein."

### 1. Implemented (all browser-verified live)
- **Standalone workspace** (`TalentSearchWorkspace.tsx`, wired in `page.tsx` for view='talent'): full-page CV-database module with own slim toolbar (back-to-Hiring-Hub, Database glyph, "CV DATABASE" badge). NO dashboard hero/stat-tiles/tabs. `RecruiterDashboard` no longer hosts a talent tab; `Header` isActive fixed (talent activates its own nav item).
- **Filters always visible** (`FilterRail.tsx`): Section defaultOpen=true — all 13 sections (Key skills, Designation, Experience, CTC, Location, Industry, Functional area, Employers, Education, Notice, Employment type, Languages, Freshness) expandable-but-open by default; browser check 13/13 visible.
- **Full-view dialog** (`CandidatePreview.tsx`): DialogContent → `w-[96vw] h-[92vh] sm:max-w-[1200px]` (measured 1200px/94%/92vh in-browser); profile tab restructured into two columns (A: stats+contact+skills+work-history; B: education+certs+languages+links+resume).
- **Similar candidates** (NEW `api/candidates/[id]/similar/route.ts` + preview tab): recruiter-gated, bounded-pool scoring = skill Jaccard 55% + role-title 15% + functional-area 12% + industry 8% + experience proximity 10%; zero-overlap+different-functional-area skipped; top 8 with match% badge, shared-skill chips, masked contacts; "View profile" pivots the dialog (keyed remount) — verified Ananya→Diya in browser.
- **Match vs JD upgrade** (`api/sourcing/match` rewritten + NEW `lib/jd-parser.ts`): two JD sources — "My posted jobs" (structured) or "Paste JD text" (AI parse via z-ai SDK → deterministic keyword fallback: lexicon scan, years regex, role/responsibility/location extraction). Scoring: must-skills 35 + role 25 + responsibilities 15 + experience 15 + good-to-have 10. **CORE GATE**: core(role+skills+responsibilities) <30% → score capped at 25, verdict CORE_MISMATCH with explicit banner — experience/location can never rescue. **Opt-in context checks** (location/notice/budget) are flag-only PASS/WARN/FAIL, never move the score.
- **E2E results**: Diya(non-maintenance) vs maintenance JD → 25/100 Core JD mismatch ✓; Chirag Pillai vs same JD → 64/100 Good fit, no gate ✓; job-record path → 19/100 vs Frontend job (honest mismatch) ✓; Chirag vs short maintenance JD + context filters → 80/100 with Location/Budget flag rows ✓; "JD parsed by: AI parser / posted job record" labels honest.
- **Bonus fixes (pre-existing bugs found via tsc)**: TalentSearch.tsx missing `advancedFilters` import + bare `booleanMode` ReferenceError in saveCurrentSearch (saved-search would crash); candidates/route.ts `parsed` null-type + missing `desiredIndustry` select; candidates/[id]/route.ts contactMasked cast. All green now.

### 2. Files
NEW: `src/components/seewee/recruiter/TalentSearchWorkspace.tsx`, `src/app/api/candidates/[id]/similar/route.ts`, `src/lib/jd-parser.ts`.
MOD: `page.tsx` (talent view → workspace), `RecruiterDashboard.tsx` (talent tab removed), `Header.tsx` (active state), `talent/FilterRail.tsx`, `talent/CandidatePreview.tsx` (size + Similar tab + JD match UI), `TalentSearch.tsx` (onOpenCandidate + key remount + 2 bug fixes), `api/sourcing/match/route.ts` (rewrite), `api/candidates/route.ts` + `[id]/route.ts` (type fixes).

### 3. Notes
- Env vars: NONE new. AI JD-parse uses existing z-ai SDK; keyword fallback is always available and labelled.
- OOM incident during dev: sandbox OOM-killed next-server once (4GB cap); restarted with `NODE_OPTIONS=--max-old-space-size=1400` + double-fork `(setsid ... &)` — stable since; all routes warmed.
- `bun run lint` green; tsc clean on all touched files; dev.log clean of 500s.

## Task ID: debrand-sourcing-v1 (2026-10-29)

**User ask (Hinglish):** "ResDex-style sourcing, or naukri.com style sourcing aisa kuch bhi na rkhe iske jagaha kuch or likhe wo competitor hai aisa na kre ki hume dikat hoga" — remove ALL competitor brand references from the product; write our own wording instead.

**Audit scope:** case-insensitive sweep of src/, scripts/, prisma/, live SQLite DB, worklog + full repo.

## Task ID: jd-search-ads-cv-v1 (2026-10-29)

**User ask (Hinglish):** (1) "view profile me candidate ka cv download krne ka options nahi hai" (2) "based on jd ai hamara khud cv search kr de aisa option search mein" (3) "jab tak search na lagaye candidate ka database na dikhe" (4) "jo wo space wha different companies apna ad kr skta hai branding kuch sochiye".

**Root cause found (CV):** only 1 of 160 candidates had cvFileName — the chip rarely rendered. Download links existed but data was missing.

**BUILT:**
- **AI JD search** (`POST /api/talent/jd-search`): paste a full JD → parsed once (AI first, deterministic fallback — shared `src/lib/jd-ai.ts` extracted from match route) → every visible candidate scored with the same transparent engine (skills 35 + role 25 + responsibilities 15 core; exp 15 + good-to-have 10 boosters) → ⛔ CORE GATE: core <30% ⇒ candidate EXCLUDED entirely (reported as screenedOut), experience/location can never rescue (explicit user rule). Optional recruiter overlays (location / min-max exp / max CTC / max notice) narrow AFTER the gate. Returns same safe payload shape as /api/candidates rows + match evidence; UsageLog SEARCH audit; quota'd.
- **JD mode UI** (TalentSearch): segmented "Keyword / Boolean" vs "AI JD match" tabs; JD textarea + 5 optional overlay inputs + "Find matching CVs"; parsed-JD summary strip (role/exp/loc chips, X of Y matched core · Z screened out · parser engine); result cards carry a scored band (percent JD match + verdict + ✓matched + ✗missing) via new `jdMatch` prop on CandidateCard; pre-search prompt, zero-hit and loading states.
- **Privacy gate**: candidate database now HIDDEN until the first explicit search (`armed` state) — no auto-run on mount; debounced filter auto-apply also gated. Landing panel replaces rail+results pre-search: headline, 3 clickable sample queries (Boolean/keyword), privacy note. "Clear all" returns to the landing (database hides again). Boolean guided builder, saved searches, within-search all arm the gate.
- **Sponsored/branding slots**: new `TalentAd` model (kind: house|sponsored, accent themes) + `GET /api/talent/ads` + seed `scripts/seed-talent-ads.ts` (1 SeeWe house ad + 3 clearly-labelled synthetic demo advertisers — Bharat Distribution Co., TechNova Solutions, SafeMove Logistics). Ad grid renders in the pre-search space (keyword landing + JD pre-search), "Sponsored"/"SeeWe" badges, accent gradients, dark-mode aware.
- **CV download in View Profile**: CandidatePreview now fetches /resume-versions and renders a dedicated "CV / Resume document" card — active version Download (teal button), profile-level /cv fallback, older-versions disclosure, and an HONEST empty state when no CV exists. Seeded generated one-page PDF CVs (`scripts/seed-candidate-cvs.ts`, hand-rolled dependency-free PDF writer) for ALL 159 candidates missing one, built from their synthetic profile data (clearly marked inside as demo).

**Fixes along the way:** import collision + dead legacy-boolean branch removed from /api/candidates (matchesBooleanQuery/legacyFlat eliminated); pre-existing tsc error in talent-search-engine parser return union fixed; dev server restarted detached (setsid) after dying.

**Verified (agent-browser + curl):** landing shows ads + privacy note, no candidate grid; sample chip → 2 results; clear-all → landing again; JD search → "17 of 156 profiles matched the JD core · 139 screened out", AI parser, score bands 77/72/72/65/65% with verdicts; profile dialog → CV card "Diya-Tiwari-CV.pdf" + Download; GET /cv → 200 application/pdf 3018B valid %PDF-1.4 containing the candidate name; lint green; dev.log clean.

## Task ID: jd-snippet-quality-v2 (2026-10-29)

**User ask (Hinglish):** JD example (Manager-HRBP) ke saath — (1) "keyword or boolean search kya kaam krega" samjhaya (no-code round), (2) "log multiple keywords bhi likh skte hai… poori profile, cv me wo chij mile to show ho" → multi-keyword + full-CV search confirm kiya aur ek missing polish add ki: keyword-ka-haunsla dikhana. (3) "ha" → build green light.

**BUILT:**
- **"Matched in CV" keyword evidence** (`/api/candidates` + CandidateCard): every positive search term now returns a contact-scrubbed ±90-char CV excerpt around its first hit (max 3/candidate) + `where` badges (designation / skills / CV). Card renders a teal "MATCHED IN CV" panel with the term highlighted inline (`<mark>` styling, dark-mode aware) and a per-term provenance chip ("PERFORMANCE · DESIGNATION + SKILLS + CV"); falls back to the plain resumeSnippet when no keyword context exists (JD mode). Type added to `TalentCandidate.matchedSnippets`.

**JD-match quality fixes (real HRBP JD exposed them):**
- AI-parsed skills are verbatim JD phrases ("performance management cycles") that never match CV tokens ("Performance Management") → `normaliseJdSkills` strips filler tails (cycles/programs/frameworks…), splits "X and Y" phrases, drops >4-word sentences and bare gerunds ("designing", "executing") with an allowlist (marketing/training/coaching…).
- **AI non-determinism made results flaky** (one run 8 matches, next 0 with junk skills) → jd-search now MERGES AI + deterministic skills (lexicon-anchored, deduped, AI first) so the parse is stable; expanded SKILL_LEXICON with 19 HR/OD terms (Change Management, Organization Development, Employee Relations, Compensation and Benefits, Total Rewards, HRBP, Succession Planning, HRMS, HRIS, Engagement Surveys, Employer Branding, Conflict Resolution…).
- `jdSkillHit` layered matcher: token/synonym → literal phrase → specific-word-in-skill-token shortcut ("compensation" hits "Compensation Benchmarking"; GENERIC_WORDS like management/development can never carry a match alone) → ≥60% word-overlap for multi-word phrases ("hr" now counts as a token). `roleTitleSimilarity` is synonym-aware ("Manager-HRBP" ↔ "HR Business Partner" = 0.5 PASS) and role extraction rejects section-header lines ("Key Responsibilities:") + picks "As a <Title>," openers.
- Verdict bands recalibrated 75/50 → 70/45 (jd-search + sourcing/match): best-available profiles against a demanding JD now label "Good fit" instead of misleading "Weak fit".

**Verified (agent-browser, ananya@technova.com):** pre-search landing = ads + privacy note, candidates hidden; keyword "performance management" → 6 candidates with MATCHED IN CV highlight (Rajat Pillai: "…Key skills: **Performance** Management…" + provenance chips); AI JD search (user's real 2.3k-char HRBP JD) → "JD read by AI parser · Role: Manager-HRBP · Exp: 10–15 yrs", "8 of 156 matched · 148 screened out", top = Pranav Kapoor 46% Good fit / Diya Gupta 45% (✓matched + ✗missing chips), JD-mode card shows CV badge; View profile → Download CV → GET /api/candidates/[id]/cv = 200 application/pdf "Pranav-Kapoor-CV.pdf" 3044B valid %PDF; unit-level controls: HRBP candidate core≈0.36 PASS, HR-Ops + TECH candidates screened out (role gate honest); lint green; dev.log clean.

## Task ID: jd-privacy-audit-v3 (2026-11-01)

**User ask (Hinglish):** "search tool kaise kaam krta hai" — explanation round; earlier "ha" green-lit the 4-feature build. Audit found the 4 features already live (built across prior review rounds), so this round = privacy-gap fixes + evidence parity + calibration.

**PRIVACY FIXES (real gaps found in audit):**
- **JD search had NO employer-block shield** (keyword search + contact reveal had it, jd-search didn't) → POST /api/talent/jd-search now loads the recruiter's company/client names and skips those candidates entirely (screenedOut++). Verified live: Diya Gupta (TechNova = Ananya's own company) never appears in her JD results — earlier "perfect match" analysis was raw-DB only; the shield is the correct product behaviour.
- **GET /api/candidates/[id]/cv had zero auth** (anyone with an id could fetch a CV) → added the resume-versions-download pattern: requesterId must be the owning candidate OR a RECRUITER/ADMIN (403 otherwise); recruiting downloads now write a CV_DOWNLOAD UsageLog row (candidateId + fileName + inline) — verified 403 on fake requesterId + audit row written.
- Recruiter CV links now pass requesterId: CandidatePreview (active version / profile fallback / older versions) + ApplicantsPanel (candidate-latest-CV inline link). Candidate-side links unchanged (owner-in-path pattern).

**EVIDENCE PARITY:**
- JD-search results now return `matchedSnippets` (same shape as keyword search): per-must-have-skill contact-scrubbed ±90-char CV excerpt + provenance chips (designation/skills/CV) → CandidateCard renders its teal "MATCHED IN CV" panel under every JD result too.

**CALIBRATION:**
- C&B synonym family added to TERM_SYNONYMS: total rewards ↔ compensation and benefits ↔ compensation benchmarking ↔ c&b (JDs say "Total Rewards", CVs say "Compensation Benchmarking" — same practice). JD scores rose (Pranav 51→55%, Rajat 47→50%); "Total Rewards" now hits the C&B-fluent pool.

**NEW (small):** "Try a sample JD" button in the AI JD match panel — fills a brand-free synthetic Senior-HRBP JD (matches the HR seed pool) so the AI parser can be felt before pasting a real JD.

**Verified (agent-browser, ananya@technova.com + curl):** pre-search landing = 4 sponsored/SeeWe ads + privacy note, 0 candidate cards; keyword "hr business partner performance management" → 6 results with MATCHED IN CV highlights; View profile → Download CV → valid %PDF-1.4 3018B; fake requesterId → 403 Forbidden; sample JD → AI parser badge + role/exp/loc chips + "6 of 157 matched · 151 screened out" + ranked 55/50/48/43/42/36% with ✓/✗ evidence + snippets; CV_DOWNLOAD audit row present; lint green; dev.log clean.

## Task ID: interview-bargein-voicecheck-v4 (2026-11-01)

**User ask (Hinglish):** video interview feedback round — (1) "answer de bolne ke baad bhi questions bolte rehta hai" — AI interviewer kept reading the question after the candidate started answering; (2) "candidate ko kiski awaj mein chahiye ye kyu puchna, ek hi rhega" — kill the voice picker, ONE fixed interviewer voice; (3) "system test kre interview se pehle" — real device test before Q1 (mic was there, speakers weren't); (4) "interview ke baad video and transcript hona chahiye recruiter dekh ske"; (5) "AI reject kyu krega, wo suggest krega — reject to recruiter krenge".

**BUILT (all five):**
- **Barge-in (the "won't stop talking" bug)**: two reactive effects stop TTS the moment the candidate starts typing (transcript non-empty while speaking) or starts recording (recording while speaking); `speak()` refuses to start and aborts mid-fetch when `recorderRef` is live; submit/skip always silences. ROOT-CAUSE FIX for the reported "AI keeps talking after Stop": `killVoice()` now bumps a `speakEpochRef` and in-flight `speak()` compares its epoch after the TTS download — a Stop pressed during the fetch can no longer be overridden when the audio arrives. Visible controls: question-card button flips Replay ⇄ "Stop voice", persona strip shows a Stop pill beside the equalizer, caption swaps to "Start answering any time — the AI stops talking automatically."
- **One fixed voice**: voice picker removed from welcome screen AND header (Select import dropped, catalog fetch/preview/persist code deleted); `AI_VOICE='jam'` const + `aiVoiceRef`; welcome card now says "Your interviewer today — one clear, steady AI voice… the moment you start answering, the AI stops talking and listens"; GET /api/tts returns the single fixed voice (POST still validates defensively).
- **Pre-interview system test**: NEW `SpeakerCheck.tsx` (shared) — plays a real TTS line in the interviewer's fixed voice via /api/tts, then asks "Did you hear it?" → Yes = PASS / No = fail with volume/tab-mute/headphone hints + Replay; wired into BOTH the logged-in welcome device check and the token-invite SystemCheck. Welcome camera teaser replaced by a LIVE camera test (auto-acquire on welcome, idempotent `startCamera` with in-flight guard + stream reuse so welcome→interview never double-acquires, live preview + "Camera ready" badge, denial still falls back honestly). Verified live: idle → PLAYING → CONFIRM → PASS.
- **Recruiter video+transcript**: already existed (InterviewHub per-answer `<video>`/`<audio>` + transcripts, media served with 206 range requests, RecordingsPanel) — verified end-to-end and retitled "Interview recording & transcript — watch each answer's video and read exactly what the candidate said, question by question."
- **AI advises, recruiter decides**: VERDICT_META labels reframed as advice ("AI suggests: Strong yes / Yes / Borderline / Not fit", ❌→⚠️) so nothing reads as an automatic rejection; AIReportCard section now "🧭 AI Suggestion — advisory only" with "the final advance / reject call always rests with the recruiter"; FinalDecisionPanel keeps the side-by-side "AI SUGGESTION — ADVISORY ONLY" vs "YOUR FINAL CALL" (Advance/Hold/Reject with confirm dialogs).

**Also found & fixed during verification:** dev server was OOM-killed + silently reaped between tool calls after sandbox restart — restarted via double-fork `( setsid … & )` which survives; a stale EADDRINUSE entry in dev.log is from a duplicate start attempt, harmless.

**Verified (agent-browser, vikram.reddy + sneha.kulkarni + ananya@technova):** Vikram resume → listening phase, AI auto-speaks, Stop voice click → "AI is speaking" gone (epoch fix proven: pre-fix the state stuck ON); Replay → speaking → Stop → Replay restored; welcome screen (fresh Sneha interview, config seeded for CloudNine job) shows NO voice picker + interviewer note + live camera-test block + mic/camera/Speaker rows; speaker test full PASS path; consent gates Begin; TEXT question: typed via prototype-setter → "AI is speaking" vanished within ~1s (typing barge-in); submit → ANSWER RECEIVED → Finish → done screen with "recordings and transcripts securely stored" + self-review; recruiter hub → Meghna detail: video player (206 media stream), transcript, "AI suggests: Not fit" chips in list + dialog, "AI ADVISES · YOU DECIDE" final panel; lint green; browser console clean.
